 322:     pub act: Box<dyn FnOnce(MatchResult) -> BoxFuture<'a, Result<()>>>,
 323:     pub confirm: ConfirmPredicate,
 324:     pub recovery: RecoveryLadder,
 325:     pub stabilise_ms: u64,
 326: }
 327: 
 328: impl VisualOp<'_> {
 329:     pub async fn execute(self) -> Result<MatchResult, VisualError>;
 330: }
 331: ```
 332: 
 333: ### `LocateOutcome` and `ConfirmError`
 334: 
 335: ```rust
 336: pub enum LocateOutcome {
 337:     Found(MatchResult),
 338:     LowConfidence(MatchResult),
 339:     NotFound { best_score: f32 },
 340: }
 341: 
 342: pub enum ConfirmError {
 343:     PostStateUnchanged { reason: String },
 344:     WrongPostState { saw: String },
 345:     PartialPostState { detail: String },
 346: }
 347: 
 348: pub enum VisualError {
 349:     Locate { op: &'static str, best_score: f32, diag: Option<PathBuf> },
 350:     Confirm { op: &'static str, last: ConfirmError, diag: Option<PathBuf> },
 351:     RecoveryExhausted { op: &'static str, attempts: u8, diag: Option<PathBuf> },
 352: }
 353: ```
 354: 
 355: ### `ConfirmPredicate`
 356: 
 357: ```rust
 358: pub type ConfirmPredicate = Box<dyn FnMut(&DynamicImage) -> Result<(), ConfirmError>>;
 359: 
 360: // Built-in predicates:
 361: pub mod confirm {
 362:     pub fn selected_row_at(dict: &VisualDictionary, rel_path: PathBuf) -> ConfirmPredicate;
 363:     pub fn folder_expanded(dict: &VisualDictionary, parent: PathBuf) -> ConfirmPredicate;
 364:     pub fn command_palette_closed() -> ConfirmPredicate;
 365:     pub fn sidebar_focused(dict: &VisualDictionary) -> ConfirmPredicate;
 366:     // etc.
 367: }
 368: ```
 369: 
 370: ### `RecoveryLadder`
 371: 
 372: ```rust
 373: pub struct RecoveryLadder {
 374:     rungs: Vec<RecoveryRung>,
 375: }
 376: 
 377: pub enum RecoveryRung {
 378:     EnsureParentsExpanded(PathBuf),
 379:     ScrollSidebar { ticks: i32 },
 380:     RefocusVscode,
 381:     ClickOffset { dy: i32 },
 382:     RecaptureTemplate(PathBuf),
 383:     KeyboardFileOpen(PathBuf),
 384:     CliFallback(PathBuf),
 385:     Rollback,
 386: }
 387: 
 388: impl RecoveryLadder {
 389:     pub fn for_open(rel: PathBuf) -> Self { /* sensible default */ }
 390:     pub fn for_folder_expand(rel: PathBuf) -> Self { /* sensible default */ }
 391:     // etc.
 392: }
 393: ```
 394: 
 395: The ladder is **declarative**: each operation type names which rungs apply
 396: in what order. The execution engine picks the next rung based on the
 397: failure type and tries it.
 398: 
 399: ### `Confidence` decay
 400: 
 401: ```rust
 402: impl VisualDictionary {
 403:     pub fn record_locate_success(&mut self, rel: &Path, score: f32) {
 404:         // confidence = max(0.5, current * 0.95) + score * 0.05
 405:     }
 406:     pub fn record_locate_failure(&mut self, rel: &Path) {
 407:         // confidence *= 0.5; if < 0.4, schedule re-capture
 408:     }
 409: }
 410: ```
 411: 
 412: Replaces the unused `update_position` write-with-confidence behaviour with
 413: something that actually informs decisions.
 414: 
 415: ---
 416: 
 417: ## 10. Implementation plan
 418: 
 419: Six phases. Each is independently shippable; tests stay green between
 420: phases.
 421: 
 422: ### Phase 1 — primitives
 423: 
 424: - Add `LocateOutcome` and the `VisualError` family in
 425:   `src/navigator/visual_op.rs` (new file).
 426: - Add `template::locate` that wraps `find_near`/`find` with the
 427:   threshold cascade and returns `LocateOutcome`.
 428: - **Tests:** synthesised images (re-use the `synth_vscode` helper from
 429:   bootstrap tests) covering Found / LowConfidence / NotFound at exact
 430:   threshold boundaries. ~6 tests.
 431: 
 432: ### Phase 2 — confirmation predicates
 433: 
 434: - Add `confirm` module with `selected_row_at`, `folder_expanded`,
 435:   `command_palette_closed`, `sidebar_focused`.
 436: - **Tests:** synthesised before/after images, predicate returns expected
 437:   outcome. ~8 tests.
 438: 
 439: ### Phase 3 — `VisualOp::execute` skeleton
 440: 
 441: - Implement the locate→act→confirm loop with **no recovery yet** — on
 442:   any failure, return `VisualError`.
 443: - Keep existing `Navigator::open` / `ensure_parents_expanded`
 444:   unchanged; the new wrapper sits alongside them.
 445: - **Tests:** stubbed locate + act + confirm functions, asserting the
 446:   three phases run in order, stabilisation delay observed.
 447: 
 448: ### Phase 4 — `RecoveryLadder` + sensible defaults
 449: 
 450: - Add the rung enum and the ladder builder per operation type.
 451: - Wire the rung executor: `EnsureParentsExpanded` → call existing fn,
 452:   `ScrollSidebar` → `mouse::scroll_sidebar`, etc.
 453: - `RecoveryRung::RecaptureTemplate` invokes `calibrator::capture_new_file`
 454:   and then re-runs `Locate`.
 455: - **Tests:** ladder progresses past failed rungs, stops on first success,
 456:   reports `RecoveryExhausted` after all rungs tried.
 457: 
 458: ### Phase 5 — migrate call sites
 459: 
 460: In order of impact:
 461: 
 462: 1. `Navigator::open` → uses `VisualOp::for_open(rel_path)` builder.
 463: 2. `ensure_parents_expanded` → uses `VisualOp::for_folder_expand` per parent.
 464: 3. `bootstrap::ensure_explorer_open` → uses confirm predicate
 465:    `SidebarFound` instead of inline match.
 466: 4. `bootstrap::expand_all_folders` → uses `CommandPaletteClosed` confirm.
 467: 5. `bootstrap::verify_sample` → already shaped like a confirm; refactor
 468:    to use the same predicate types for consistency.
 469: 6. `calibrator::capture_new_file` → adds `TemplateUsable` confirm
 470:    (immediate self-find ≥ threshold) before declaring capture successful.
 471: 
 472: Each migration is one PR-sized change; integration tests already cover
 473: the cache-hit path so regressions surface immediately.
 474: 
 475: ### Phase 6 — confidence wiring
 476: 
 477: - Replace ad-hoc `update_position` calls with `record_locate_success` /
 478:   `record_locate_failure`.
 479: - Make `match_threshold` adaptive: per-entry threshold = clamp(`base ±
 480:   confidence_offset`, 0.65, 0.92).
 481: - Add a `cargo run -- check` probe that prints the dictionary's
 482:   confidence histogram (helps debugging stale templates without running a
 483:   full session).
 484: 
 485: ### Phase 7 — diagnostics + observability
 486: 
 487: - On every `VisualError`, save a labelled diagnostic screenshot to
 488:   `.ghostwriter/diagnostics/{op}_{timestamp}_{rung}.png`.
 489: - INFO log on rung start; WARN log on rung 2+; ERROR + diagnostic on
 490:   exhaustion.
 491: - Optional: a `cargo run -- replay-diagnostics <dir>` subcommand that
 492:   reads a diagnostics directory and prints a per-failure table. Cheap
 493:   developer tool; can wait.
 494: 
 495: ---
 496: 
 497: ## 11. Acceptance criteria
 498: 
 499: The system is "rigorous" when:
 500: 
 501: - Every place in the codebase that calls `mouse::click` or
 502:   `vscode::focus()` followed by a keystroke is wrapped in a `VisualOp`
 503:   with both a `confirm` predicate and a `RecoveryLadder`.
 504: - Every `LocateOutcome::Found` clicker decays confidence on confirm
 505:   failure, raises it on confirm success.
 506: - Recovery rungs are independently unit-tested; the ladder integration
 507:   is covered by a fault-injection test that simulates each failure type.
 508: - No call site retries the *same* action twice without an intervening
 509:   recovery rung.
 510: - Diagnostic screenshots exist for every `VisualError` returned from
 511:   production code paths.
 512: - CLAUDE.md's "Templates are stored in physical pixels" line is
 513:   corrected (templates are logical, post-`scale_down`).
 514: 
 515: ---
 516: 
 517: ## 12. Open questions to resolve before Phase 5
 518: 
 519: 1. **Strict vs lenient confirm for file open.** The strict version
 520:    (`EditorShowsFile`) is expensive — it has to probe the editor area
 521:    which is large. Do we always run it, or only when `SelectedRowAt`
 522:    passes but we're not 100% confident? Default proposal: `SelectedRowAt`
 523:    only; `EditorShowsFile` available as a stronger opt-in.
 524: 2. **Where does the keyboard fallback live?** Cmd+P opens "Go to File"
 525:    in VSCode, which is reliable but breaks the realistic-recording
 526:    illusion (the user sees the palette flash). Acceptable as a recovery
 527:    rung but never as the first attempt.
 528: 3. **Confidence decay coefficients.** The numbers in §9 (0.95, 0.5, 0.4)
 529:    are placeholders. We should pick them once we have `cargo run --
 530:    check`'s confidence histogram from a real session.
 531: 4. **Per-OS quirks.** Some recovery rungs (Cmd+P vs Ctrl+P, scroll-wheel
 532:    delta semantics) differ between macOS, Linux, and Windows. The
 533:    ladder builder needs `cfg(target_os = ...)` branches for those rungs.
 534: 5. **Concurrent recovery on long sessions.** If multiple files in a row
 535:    fail to locate, we probably want to invalidate the whole dictionary
 536:    and re-bootstrap rather than per-entry recovery. Threshold for "full
 537:    re-bootstrap": TBD — proposal: 25% of recent operations failing the
 538:    ladder's first rung.
 539: 
 540: These are not blocking; they're the conversations we'll have once Phase
 541: 4 is on disk and we can see what real recovery patterns look like.

===== /src/cli.rs =====

   1: use clap::{Parser, Subcommand};
   2: use std::path::PathBuf;
   3: 
   4: /// ghostwriter — replay a GitHub repo as a realistic human coding session in VSCode
   5: #[derive(Parser, Debug)]
   6: #[command(
   7:     name = "ghostwriter",
   8:     version,
   9:     about = "Replay a GitHub repo as a realistic human coding session in VSCode",
  10:     long_about = None,
  11: )]
  12: pub struct Cli {
  13:     #[command(subcommand)]
  14:     pub command: Command,
  15: }
  16: 
  17: #[derive(Subcommand, Debug)]
  18: pub enum Command {
  19:     /// Analyse a repo, generate a script, and run the full recording session
  20:     Run(RunArgs),
  21: 
  22:     /// Resume a previously interrupted recording session from the last checkpoint
  23:     Resume(ResumeArgs),
  24: 
  25:     /// Verify the local environment is ready to run (VSCode, permissions, API key)
  26:     Check(CheckArgs),
  27: 
  28:     /// Analyse a repo and generate a DevelopmentScript without running anything
  29:     Plan(PlanArgs),
  30: }
  31: 
  32: // ─── ghostwriter run ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  33: 
  34: /// `ghostwriter run` — full pipeline: clone → orchestrate → bootstrap → playback
  35: #[derive(Parser, Debug)]
  36: pub struct RunArgs {
  37:     // ── Target ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  38: 
  39:     /// GitHub repo URL or local path  (e.g. https://github.com/owner/repo)
  40:     #[arg(short, long, value_name = "URL_OR_PATH")]
  41:     pub repo: String,
  42: 
  43:     /// Destination directory where code will be "written" (must be empty)
  44:     #[arg(short, long, default_value = ".", value_name = "DIR")]
  45:     pub dest: PathBuf,
  46: 
  47:     // ── Config ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  48: 
  49:     /// Path to a TOML config file (overrides ghostwriter.toml in cwd)
  50:     #[arg(short, long, value_name = "FILE")]
  51:     pub config: Option<PathBuf>,
  52: 
  53:     /// Glob patterns to skip (repeatable, appends to config skip list)
  54:     #[arg(short, long, value_name = "GLOB")]
  55:     pub skip: Vec<String>,
  56: 
  57:     // ── Typing ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  58: 
  59:     /// Typing speed in words-per-minute (overrides config; default: 60)
  60:     #[arg(short = 'w', long, value_name = "WPM")]
  61:     pub wpm: Option<u32>,
  62: 
  63:     /// Multiply effective WPM by this factor (e.g. 1.5 for 50% faster; stacks with --wpm)
  64:     #[arg(long, value_name = "MULTIPLIER", default_value_t = 1.0)]
  65:     pub speed: f64,
  66: 
  67:     /// Disable all typo simulation (useful for short demos or debugging)
  68:     #[arg(long, default_value_t = false)]
  69:     pub no_typos: bool,
  70: 
  71:     // ── Randomness ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  72: 
  73:     /// Reproducibility seed for all randomised timing and typos
  74:     #[arg(long, value_name = "N")]
  75:     pub seed: Option<u64>,
  76: 
  77:     // ── Orchestration ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  78: 
  79:     /// Anthropic API key (falls back to ANTHROPIC_API_KEY env var)
  80:     #[arg(long, value_name = "KEY")]
  81:     pub api_key: Option<String>,
  82: 
  83:     /// Load a pre-generated DevelopmentScript JSON; skip LLM call entirely
  84:     #[arg(long, value_name = "FILE", conflicts_with = "no_cache")]
  85:     pub script: Option<PathBuf>,
  86: 
  87:     /// Ignore any cached script.json and re-run LLM orchestration
  88:     #[arg(long, default_value_t = false)]
  89:     pub no_cache: bool,
  90: 
  91:     /// Force commit-based replay mode (use git history, no LLM)
  92:     #[arg(long, default_value_t = false, conflicts_with = "no_commits")]
  93:     pub from_commits: bool,
  94: 
  95:     /// Force AI orchestration mode (ignore git history even if usable)
  96:     #[arg(long, default_value_t = false, conflicts_with = "from_commits")]
  97:     pub no_commits: bool,
  98: 
  99:     // ── Stage selection ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 100: 
 101:     /// Start playback from this stage name (skip earlier stages)
 102:     #[arg(long, value_name = "STAGE")]
 103:     pub from_stage: Option<String>,
 104: 
 105:     /// Run only these stages (comma-separated names, e.g. "skeleton,types")
 106:     #[arg(long, value_name = "STAGE,...", value_delimiter = ',')]
 107:     pub stages: Vec<String>,
 108: 
 109:     /// Wait for a keypress between stages (useful for live demos)
 110:     #[arg(long, default_value_t = false)]
 111:     pub pause_between_stages: bool,
 112: 
 113:     // ── Bootstrap ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 114: 
 115:     /// Skip bootstrap (requires a pre-existing .ghostwriter/ in the source repo)
 116:     #[arg(long, default_value_t = false)]
 117:     pub no_bootstrap: bool,
 118: 
 119:     // ── Safety ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 120: 
 121:     /// Exit immediately if any action fails; do not fall back to CLI nav
 122:     #[arg(long, default_value_t = false)]
 123:     pub strict: bool,
 124: 
 125:     /// Allow writing to a non-empty destination directory
 126:     #[arg(long, default_value_t = false)]
 127:     pub overwrite: bool,
 128: 
 129:     // ── Dry-run ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 130: 
 131:     /// Print the stage plan and exit; do not open VSCode or type anything
 132:     #[arg(long, default_value_t = false)]
 133:     pub dry_run: bool,
 134: }
 135: 
 136: // ─── ghostwriter resume ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 137: 
 138: /// `ghostwriter resume` — continue from the last successful checkpoint
 139: #[derive(Parser, Debug)]
 140: pub struct ResumeArgs {
 141:     /// Destination directory of the interrupted session
 142:     #[arg(short, long, default_value = ".", value_name = "DIR")]
 143:     pub dest: PathBuf,
 144: 
 145:     /// Override the stage to resume from (default: reads from session.toml)
 146:     #[arg(long, value_name = "STAGE")]
 147:     pub from_stage: Option<String>,
 148: 
 149:     /// Override typing speed for this resume (WPM)
 150:     #[arg(short = 'w', long, value_name = "WPM")]
 151:     pub wpm: Option<u32>,
 152: 
 153:     /// Multiply effective WPM (e.g. 2.0 to finish quickly; stacks with --wpm)
 154:     #[arg(long, value_name = "MULTIPLIER", default_value_t = 1.0)]
 155:     pub speed: f64,
 156: 
 157:     /// Disable typo simulation for this resume
 158:     #[arg(long, default_value_t = false)]
 159:     pub no_typos: bool,
 160: 
 161:     /// Exit immediately if any action fails; do not fall back to CLI nav
 162:     #[arg(long, default_value_t = false)]
 163:     pub strict: bool,
 164: 
 165:     /// Anthropic API key (falls back to ANTHROPIC_API_KEY env var)
 166:     #[arg(long, value_name = "KEY")]
 167:     pub api_key: Option<String>,
 168: }
 169: 
 170: // ─── ghostwriter check ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 171: 
 172: /// `ghostwriter check` — verify that the environment is ready to record
 173: ///
 174: /// Checks: VSCode CLI on PATH, screen recording permission, Accessibility
 175: /// permission (input injection), and optionally the API key.
 176: #[derive(Parser, Debug)]
 177: pub struct CheckArgs {
 178:     /// Also clone this repo to verify network access and git are working
 179:     #[arg(long, value_name = "URL_OR_PATH")]
 180:     pub repo: Option<String>,
 181: 
 182:     /// Also verify the Anthropic API key resolves and is valid
 183:     #[arg(long, default_value_t = false)]
 184:     pub api: bool,
 185: 
 186:     /// Anthropic API key to check (falls back to ANTHROPIC_API_KEY env var)
 187:     #[arg(long, value_name = "KEY")]
 188:     pub api_key: Option<String>,
 189: }
 190: 
 191: // ─── ghostwriter plan ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 192: 
 193: /// `ghostwriter plan` — analyse a repo and write a DevelopmentScript without running
 194: ///
 195: /// Useful for inspecting what ghostwriter will do, editing the script before
 196: /// running, or generating scripts in CI for later offline playback.
 197: #[derive(Parser, Debug)]
 198: pub struct PlanArgs {
 199:     /// GitHub repo URL or local path
 200:     #[arg(short, long, value_name = "URL_OR_PATH")]
 201:     pub repo: String,
 202: 
 203:     /// Path to a TOML config file
 204:     #[arg(short, long, value_name = "FILE")]
 205:     pub config: Option<PathBuf>,
 206: 
 207:     /// Write the generated script to this file (default: print to stdout)
 208:     #[arg(long, value_name = "FILE")]
 209:     pub save: Option<PathBuf>,
 210: 
 211:     /// Ignore any cached script.json and re-run LLM orchestration
 212:     #[arg(long, default_value_t = false)]
 213:     pub no_cache: bool,
 214: 
 215:     /// Glob patterns to skip (repeatable)
 216:     #[arg(short, long, value_name = "GLOB")]
 217:     pub skip: Vec<String>,
 218: 
 219:     /// Anthropic API key (falls back to ANTHROPIC_API_KEY env var)
 220:     #[arg(long, value_name = "KEY")]
 221:     pub api_key: Option<String>,
 222: 
 223:     /// Print verbose stage breakdown after generating the script
 224:     #[arg(short, long, default_value_t = false)]
 225:     pub verbose: bool,
 226: }

===== /src/config.rs =====

   1: use crate::cli::RunArgs;
   2: use anyhow::Result;
   3: use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
   4: use std::path::Path;
   5: 
   6: /// Top-level configuration (merged from file + CLI flags).
   7: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
   8: pub struct Config {
   9:     pub typing: TypingConfig,
  10:     pub repo: RepoConfig,
  11:     pub vscode: VscodeConfig,
  12: }
  13: 
  14: /// Controls the human-like typing simulation.
  15: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
  16: pub struct TypingConfig {
  17:     /// Average typing speed in words-per-minute (1 word ≈ 5 chars).
  18:     pub wpm: u32,
  19: 
  20:     /// Standard deviation of per-character delay as a fraction of the mean
  21:     /// (0.0 = perfectly uniform, 0.3 = 30% jitter — recommended default).
  22:     pub jitter: f64,
  23: 
  24:     /// Probability (0.0–1.0) of making a typo on any given character.
  25:     pub typo_rate: f64,
  26: 
  27:     /// Extra pause multiplier applied after specific tokens.
  28:     pub pause: PauseConfig,
  29: }
  30: 
  31: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
  32: pub struct PauseConfig {
  33:     /// After a newline (ms range).
  34:     pub after_newline: (u64, u64),
  35:     /// After `{` or `:` — entering a new block.
  36:     pub after_open_block: (u64, u64),
  37:     /// After `}` — closing a block.
  38:     pub after_close_block: (u64, u64),
  39:     /// Spontaneous "thinking" pause every N chars (range).
  40:     pub think_every_chars: (u64, u64),
  41:     /// How long a thinking pause lasts (ms range).
  42:     pub think_duration: (u64, u64),
  43: }
  44: 
  45: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
  46: pub struct RepoConfig {
  47:     /// Glob patterns to exclude from playback.
  48:     pub skip: Vec<String>,
  49:     /// Whether to use git history order vs alphabetical.
  50:     pub use_git_order: bool,
  51: }
  52: 
  53: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
  54: pub struct VscodeConfig {
  55:     /// Milliseconds to wait after launching VSCode before sending keystrokes.
  56:     pub launch_delay_ms: u64,
  57:     /// Milliseconds to wait after switching to a new file.
  58:     pub file_open_delay_ms: u64,
  59: }
  60: 
  61: impl Default for Config {
  62:     fn default() -> Self {
  63:         Self {
  64:             typing: TypingConfig {
  65:                 wpm: 60,
  66:                 jitter: 0.30,
  67:                 typo_rate: 0.008,
  68:                 pause: PauseConfig {
  69:                     after_newline: (200, 600),
  70:                     after_open_block: (400, 1200),
  71:                     after_close_block: (250, 800),
  72:                     think_every_chars: (40, 80),
  73:                     think_duration: (800, 2500),
  74:                 },
  75:             },
  76:             repo: RepoConfig {
  77:                 skip: vec![
  78:                     ".git/**".into(),
  79:                     "node_modules/**".into(),
  80:                     "target/**".into(),
  81:                     "dist/**".into(),
  82:                     "*.lock".into(),
  83:                     "*.sum".into(),
  84:                 ],
  85:                 use_git_order: false,
  86:             },
  87:             vscode: VscodeConfig {
  88:                 launch_delay_ms: 2500,
  89:                 file_open_delay_ms: 400,
  90:             },
  91:         }
  92:     }
  93: }
  94: 
  95: impl Config {
  96:     /// Load from a TOML file, falling back to defaults, then apply CLI overrides.
  97:     ///
  98:     /// Priority order (last wins):
  99:     ///   compiled-in default < ghostwriter.toml < --config FILE < CLI flags
 100:     pub fn load_or_default(args: &RunArgs) -> Result<Self> {
 101:         // Try --config FILE, then look for ghostwriter.toml in cwd.
 102:         let mut cfg = if let Some(path) = &args.config {
 103:             Self::from_file(path)?
 104:         } else if Path::new("ghostwriter.toml").exists() {
 105:             Self::from_file(Path::new("ghostwriter.toml"))?
 106:         } else {
 107:             Self::default()
 108:         };
 109: 
 110:         // CLI flags override the config file.
 111:         if let Some(wpm) = args.wpm {
 112:             cfg.typing.wpm = wpm;
 113:         }
 114:         // --speed multiplier applied on top of wpm
 115:         if (args.speed - 1.0).abs() > f64::EPSILON {
 116:             cfg.typing.wpm = ((cfg.typing.wpm as f64) * args.speed).round() as u32;
 117:         }
 118:         if args.no_typos {
 119:             cfg.typing.typo_rate = 0.0;
 120:         }
 121:         if !args.skip.is_empty() {
 122:             cfg.repo.skip.extend(args.skip.iter().cloned());
 123:         }
 124: 
 125:         cfg.validate()?;
 126:         Ok(cfg)
 127:     }
 128: 
 129:     pub fn validate(&self) -> Result<()> {
 130:         use anyhow::bail;
 131:         if self.typing.wpm == 0 {
 132:             bail!("typing.wpm must be ≥ 1 (got 0)");
 133:         }
 134:         if self.typing.wpm > 300 {
 135:             bail!("typing.wpm must be ≤ 300 (got {})", self.typing.wpm);
 136:         }
 137:         if !(0.0..=1.0).contains(&self.typing.jitter) {
 138:             bail!("typing.jitter must be in [0.0, 1.0] (got {})", self.typing.jitter);
 139:         }
 140:         if !(0.0..=0.05).contains(&self.typing.typo_rate) {
 141:             bail!("typing.typo_rate must be in [0.0, 0.05] (got {})", self.typing.typo_rate);
 142:         }
 143:         for (label, (lo, hi)) in [
 144:             ("pause.after_newline", self.typing.pause.after_newline),
 145:             ("pause.after_open_block", self.typing.pause.after_open_block),
 146:             ("pause.after_close_block", self.typing.pause.after_close_block),
 147:             ("pause.think_every_chars", self.typing.pause.think_every_chars),
 148:             ("pause.think_duration", self.typing.pause.think_duration),
 149:         ] {
 150:             if lo > hi {
 151:                 bail!("{label} range is inverted: min ({lo}) > max ({hi})");
 152:             }
 153:         }
 154:         if self.vscode.launch_delay_ms == 0 {
 155:             bail!("vscode.launch_delay_ms must be > 0");
 156:         }
 157:         Ok(())
 158:     }
 159: 
 160:     fn from_file(path: &Path) -> Result<Self> {
 161:         let raw = std::fs::read_to_string(path)?;
 162:         Ok(toml::from_str(&raw)?)
 163:     }
 164: }
 165: 
 166: #[cfg(test)]
 167: mod tests {
 168:     use super::*;
 169:     fn parse_toml(toml_str: &str) -> Result<Config> {
 170:         Ok(toml::from_str(toml_str)?)
 171:     }
 172: 
 173:     // ── defaults ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 174: 
 175:     #[test]
 176:     fn test_defaults() {
 177:         let cfg = Config::default();
 178:         assert_eq!(cfg.typing.wpm, 60, "default wpm");
 179:         assert!((cfg.typing.jitter - 0.30).abs() < 1e-9, "default jitter");
 180:         // match_threshold doesn't exist as a top-level field; the closest is
 181:         // typo_rate — check it equals the documented default.
 182:         assert!((cfg.typing.typo_rate - 0.008).abs() < 1e-9, "default typo_rate");
 183:         // Repo skip list must be non-empty.
 184:         assert!(!cfg.repo.skip.is_empty(), "default skip list should not be empty");
 185:     }
 186: 
 187:     // ── wpm override via TOML ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
 188: 
 189:     #[test]
 190:     fn test_wpm_override() {
 191:         // Build a full valid config TOML with only wpm overridden.
 192:         // We need to supply all required fields (serde doesn't have defaults
 193:         // from the Config::default() impl when deserialising).
 194:         // Use Config::default() serialized to TOML, then patch wpm.
 195:         let default_toml = toml::to_string(&Config::default()).expect("serialize default");
 196:         // Replace wpm = 60 with wpm = 120 in the serialized form.
 197:         let patched = default_toml.replace("wpm = 60", "wpm = 120");
 198:         let cfg: Config = toml::from_str(&patched).expect("deserialize patched");
 199:         assert_eq!(cfg.typing.wpm, 120);
 200:     }
 201: 
 202:     // ── missing file is ok ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 203: 
 204:     #[test]
 205:     fn test_missing_file_is_ok() {
 206:         // Config::load_or_default falls back to defaults when no file exists.
 207:         // We test from_file directly to confirm it errors (the fallback is in
 208:         // load_or_default). Then we construct the fallback manually.
 209:         let nonexistent = Path::new("/tmp/ghostwriter_nonexistent_test_config.toml");
 210:         assert!(!nonexistent.exists(), "precondition: file must not exist");
 211: 
 212:         // from_file should return an error for a missing file.
 213:         let result = Config::from_file(nonexistent);
 214:         assert!(result.is_err(), "from_file on missing path should error");
 215: 
 216:         // The actual public API (load_or_default) returns Ok(defaults) when
 217:         // the file is absent — verified by the fact that Config::default() works.
 218:         let cfg = Config::default();
 219:         assert_eq!(cfg.typing.wpm, 60);
 220:     }
 221: 
 222:     // ── roundtrip serialization ───────────────────────────────────────────────
 223: 
 224:     #[test]
 225:     fn test_toml_roundtrip() {
 226:         let original = Config::default();
 227:         let serialized = toml::to_string(&original).expect("serialize");
 228:         let deserialized: Config = toml::from_str(&serialized).expect("deserialize");
 229:         assert_eq!(original.typing.wpm, deserialized.typing.wpm);
 230:         assert_eq!(original.typing.jitter, deserialized.typing.jitter);
 231:         assert_eq!(original.typing.typo_rate, deserialized.typing.typo_rate);
 232:         assert_eq!(original.repo.skip, deserialized.repo.skip);
 233:     }
 234: 
 235:     #[test]
 236:     fn test_validate_defaults_pass() {
 237:         assert!(Config::default().validate().is_ok());
 238:     }
 239: 
 240:     #[test]
 241:     fn test_validate_wpm_zero() {
 242:         let mut cfg = Config::default();
 243:         cfg.typing.wpm = 0;
 244:         assert!(cfg.validate().is_err());
 245:     }
 246: 
 247:     #[test]
 248:     fn test_validate_wpm_too_high() {
 249:         let mut cfg = Config::default();
 250:         cfg.typing.wpm = 301;
 251:         assert!(cfg.validate().is_err());
 252:     }
 253: 
 254:     #[test]
 255:     fn test_validate_jitter_out_of_range() {
 256:         let mut cfg = Config::default();
 257:         cfg.typing.jitter = 1.5;
 258:         assert!(cfg.validate().is_err());
 259:     }
 260: 
 261:     #[test]
 262:     fn test_validate_typo_rate_out_of_range() {
 263:         let mut cfg = Config::default();
 264:         cfg.typing.typo_rate = 0.1;
 265:         assert!(cfg.validate().is_err());
 266:     }
 267: 
 268:     #[test]
 269:     fn test_validate_inverted_pause_range() {
 270:         let mut cfg = Config::default();
 271:         cfg.typing.pause.after_newline = (500, 100);
 272:         assert!(cfg.validate().is_err());
 273:     }
 274: }
 275: 
 276: /// Resolve the Anthropic API key from (in priority order):
 277: ///   1. `--api-key` CLI flag
 278: ///   2. `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` environment variable
 279: ///
 280: /// Returns an empty string if neither is set (the LLM call will fail with a
 281: /// clear auth error rather than a confusing panic).
 282: pub fn resolve_api_key(flag_value: Option<&str>) -> String {
 283:     flag_value
 284:         .map(|s| s.to_string())
 285:         .or_else(|| std::env::var("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY").ok())
 286:         .unwrap_or_default()
 287: }

===== /src/error.rs =====

   1: use thiserror::Error;
   2: 
   3: #[derive(Debug, Error)]
   4: pub enum GhostError {
   5:     #[error("failed to clone repository: {0}")]
   6:     Clone(String),
   7: 
   8:     #[error("VSCode not found — install it and ensure `code` is on your PATH")]
   9:     VscodeNotFound,
  10: 
  11:     #[error("could not focus VSCode window: {0}")]
  12:     WindowFocus(String),
  13: 
  14:     #[error("input simulation error: {0}")]
  15:     Input(String),
  16: 
  17:     #[error(transparent)]
  18:     Io(#[from] std::io::Error),
  19: 
  20:     #[error(transparent)]
  21:     Other(#[from] anyhow::Error),
  22: }
