===== /src/main.rs =====

   1: mod cli;
   2: mod config;
   3: mod error;
   4: mod navigator;
   5: mod orchestrator;
   6: mod repo;
   7: mod session;
   8: mod typer;
   9: 
  10: use anyhow::{bail, Result};
  11: use clap::Parser;
  12: use cli::{Cli, Command};
  13: use indicatif::{ProgressBar, ProgressStyle};
  14: use tracing::info;
  15: 
  16: #[tokio::main]
  17: async fn main() -> Result<()> {
  18:     tracing_subscriber::fmt()
  19:         .with_env_filter(
  20:             std::env::var("GHOSTWRITER_LOG")
  21:                 .unwrap_or_else(|_| "ghostwriter=info".to_string()),
  22:         )
  23:         .init();
  24: 
  25:     let cli = Cli::parse();
  26: 
  27:     match cli.command {
  28:         Command::Run(args) => cmd_run(args).await,
  29:         Command::Resume(args) => cmd_resume(args).await,
  30:         Command::Check(args) => cmd_check(args).await,
  31:         Command::Plan(args) => cmd_plan(args).await,
  32:     }
  33: }
  34: 
  35: // ─── ghostwriter run ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  36: 
  37: async fn cmd_run(args: cli::RunArgs) -> Result<()> {
  38:     let cfg = config::Config::load_or_default(&args)?;
  39:     let api_key = config::resolve_api_key(args.api_key.as_deref());
  40: 
  41:     // Guard: destination must be empty unless --overwrite
  42:     if !args.overwrite && args.dest.exists() {
  43:         let is_empty = args.dest.read_dir().map(|mut d| d.next().is_none()).unwrap_or(false);
  44:         if !is_empty {
  45:             bail!(
  46:                 "destination '{}' is not empty. Use --overwrite to allow writing to a non-empty directory.",
  47:                 args.dest.display()
  48:             );
  49:         }
  50:     }
  51: 
  52:     info!("ghostwriter run");
  53:     info!("repo:  {}", args.repo);
  54:     info!("dest:  {}", args.dest.display());
  55:     info!("speed: {} WPM", cfg.typing.wpm);
  56: 
  57:     // 1. Fetch / clone the repository
  58:     let source_root = repo::fetch(&args.repo, &cfg).await?;
  59:     info!("repo ready at {}", source_root.display());
  60: 
  61:     // 2. Orchestrate — produce a DevelopmentScript
  62:     let script = if let Some(script_path) = &args.script {
  63:         info!("loading pre-generated script from {}", script_path.display());
  64:         orchestrator::script::DevelopmentScript::load(script_path)?
  65:     } else {
  66:         match pick_orchestration_mode(&args, &source_root) {
  67:             OrchestrationMode::Commits => {
  68:                 info!("commit-based orchestration");
  69:                 let commits = repo::history::load_history(&source_root)?;
  70:                 info!("loaded {} commits", commits.len());
  71:                 let project_name = source_root
  72:                     .file_name()
  73:                     .and_then(|n| n.to_str())
  74:                     .unwrap_or("project")
  75:                     .to_string();
  76:                 orchestrator::commit_planner::plan_from_commits(&commits, &project_name)
  77:             }
  78:             OrchestrationMode::Ai => {
  79:                 info!("AI orchestration");
  80:                 let cache_path = if args.no_cache {
  81:                     None
  82:                 } else {
  83:                     Some(source_root.join(".ghostwriter").join("script.json"))
  84:                 };
  85:                 orchestrator::orchestrate(&source_root, cache_path.as_deref(), &cfg, &api_key)
  86:                     .await?
  87:             }
  88:         }
  89:     };
  90: 
  91:     // Dry-run: print plan and exit
  92:     if args.dry_run {
  93:         println!("Stage plan for '{}':", args.repo);
  94:         for (i, stage) in script.stages.iter().enumerate() {
  95:             println!("  {:2}. {} — {} actions", i + 1, stage.name, stage.actions.len());
  96:         }
  97:         return Ok(());
  98:     }
  99: 
 100:     // Collect stage names for checkpointing
 101:     let all_stage_names: Vec<String> = script.stages.iter().map(|s| s.name.clone()).collect();
 102: 
 103:     // 3. Initialise session state
 104:     let script_path = source_root.join(".ghostwriter").join("script.json");
 105:     let mut session = session::SessionState::new(
 106:         &args.repo,
 107:         args.dest.clone(),
 108:         source_root.clone(),
 109:         script_path,
 110:         args.seed,
 111:         cfg.typing.wpm,
 112:         args.no_typos,
 113:         &all_stage_names,
 114:     );
 115:     session.save()?;
 116: 
 117:     // Determine the starting stage
 118:     let from_stage = args.from_stage.as_deref();
 119: 
 120:     // 4. Bootstrap — run on the COMPLETE source repo
 121:     let mut nav = navigator::Navigator::bootstrap(&script, &source_root, &args.dest).await?;
 122:     info!("navigator ready ({} px row height)", nav.layout.row_height_px);
 123: 
 124:     // 5. Open VSCode at the destination for playback
 125:     typer::vscode::open(&args.dest)?;
 126:     tokio::time::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(cfg.vscode.launch_delay_ms)).await;
 127: 
 128:     // 6. Execute the script stage-by-stage with checkpointing
 129:     run_stages(&script, &args.dest, &cfg, args.seed, &mut session, from_stage, &all_stage_names, args.pause_between_stages, &mut nav).await?;
 130: 
 131:     info!("done");
 132:     Ok(())
 133: }
 134: 
 135: // ─── ghostwriter resume ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 136: 
 137: async fn cmd_resume(args: cli::ResumeArgs) -> Result<()> {
 138:     if !session::SessionState::exists(&args.dest) {
 139:         bail!(
 140:             "no session file found at '{}'.\n\
 141:              Run 'ghostwriter run' first to start a new session.",
 142:             args.dest.join(".ghostwriter").join("session.toml").display()
 143:         );
 144:     }
 145: 
 146:     let mut session = session::SessionState::load(&args.dest)?;
 147: 
 148:     if session.checkpoint.status == session::SessionStatus::Completed {
 149:         bail!(
 150:             "session '{}' is already complete (all stages finished).\n\
 151:              Start a new session with 'ghostwriter run'.",
 152:             session.session.id
 153:         );
 154:     }
 155: 
 156:     info!("resuming session '{}' for '{}'", session.session.id, session.session.repo);
 157: 
 158:     // CLI overrides for this resume.
 159:     // Resolve as an owned String so we can use it in both dummy_run_args and run_stages.
 160:     let from_stage_owned: Option<String> = session
 161:         .resume_from_stage(args.from_stage.as_deref())
 162:         .map(|s| s.to_string());
 163: 
 164:     match &from_stage_owned {
 165:         Some(stage) => info!("resuming from stage: {}", stage),
 166:         None => bail!("no stage to resume — session may already be complete"),
 167:     }
 168: 
 169:     // Load the original config (from source root or cwd)
 170:     let dummy_run_args = cli::RunArgs {
 171:         repo: session.session.repo.clone(),
 172:         dest: args.dest.clone(),
 173:         config: None,
 174:         skip: Vec::new(),
 175:         wpm: args.wpm,
 176:         speed: args.speed,
 177:         no_typos: args.no_typos,
 178:         seed: session.run.seed,
 179:         api_key: args.api_key.clone(),
 180:         script: Some(session.run.script_path.clone()),
 181:         no_cache: false,
 182:         from_stage: from_stage_owned.clone(),
 183:         stages: Vec::new(),
 184:         pause_between_stages: false,
 185:         no_bootstrap: false,
 186:         strict: args.strict,
 187:         overwrite: true,
 188:         dry_run: false,
 189:         from_commits: false,
 190:         no_commits: false,
 191:     };
 192:     let from_stage = from_stage_owned.as_deref();
 193: 
 194:     let cfg = config::Config::load_or_default(&dummy_run_args)?;
 195:     let api_key = config::resolve_api_key(args.api_key.as_deref());
 196: 
 197:     // Reload the cached script
 198:     let script = orchestrator::orchestrate(
 199:         &session.run.source_root,
 200:         Some(&session.run.script_path),
 201:         &cfg,
 202:         &api_key,
 203:     ).await?;
 204: 
 205:     let all_stage_names: Vec<String> = script.stages.iter().map(|s| s.name.clone()).collect();
 206: 
 207:     // Re-bootstrap the navigator (needed to re-establish visual dictionary)
 208:     info!("re-bootstrapping navigator…");
 209:     let mut nav = navigator::Navigator::bootstrap(&script, &session.run.source_root, &args.dest).await?;
 210: 
 211:     // Ensure VSCode is open at the destination
 212:     typer::vscode::open(&args.dest)?;
 213:     tokio::time::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(cfg.vscode.launch_delay_ms)).await;
 214: 
 215:     run_stages(&script, &args.dest, &cfg, session.run.seed, &mut session, from_stage, &all_stage_names, false, &mut nav).await?;
 216: 
 217:     info!("done");
 218:     Ok(())
 219: }
 220: 
 221: // ─── ghostwriter check ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 222: 
 223: async fn cmd_check(args: cli::CheckArgs) -> Result<()> {
 224:     use std::process::Command as Proc;
 225: 
 226:     let mut all_ok = true;
 227: 
 228:     // 1. VSCode CLI
 229:     let code_ok = Proc::new("which").arg("code").output().map(|o| o.status.success()).unwrap_or(false);
 230:     if code_ok {
 231:         println!("  ✓ VSCode CLI (code) found on PATH");
 232:     } else {
 233:         println!("  ✗ VSCode CLI not found — install via VSCode Command Palette → 'Install code command in PATH'");
 234:         all_ok = false;
 235:     }
 236: 
 237:     // 2. Screen capture (non-black frame)
 238:     match navigator::screen::capture_screen() {
 239:         Ok(img) => {
 240:             if navigator::screen::is_all_black(&img) {
 241:                 println!("  ✗ Screen Recording permission denied — grant in System Settings → Privacy & Security → Screen Recording");
 242:                 all_ok = false;
 243:             } else {
 244:                 println!("  ✓ Screen Recording permission granted");
 245:             }
 246:         }
 247:         Err(e) => {
 248:             println!("  ✗ Screen capture failed: {e}");
 249:             all_ok = false;
 250:         }
 251:     }
 252: 
 253:     // 3. Accessibility (input injection)
 254:     let injection_ok = tokio::task::spawn_blocking(|| {
 255:         use enigo::{Enigo, Key, Keyboard, Settings};
 256:         let mut e = match Enigo::new(&Settings::default()) {
 257:             Ok(e) => e,
 258:             Err(_) => return false,
 259:         };
 260:         e.key(Key::Shift, enigo::Direction::Press).is_ok()
 261:             && e.key(Key::Shift, enigo::Direction::Release).is_ok()
 262:     })
 263:     .await
 264:     .unwrap_or(false);
 265:     if injection_ok {
 266:         println!("  ✓ Accessibility permission granted (input injection works)");
 267:     } else {
 268:         println!("  ✗ Accessibility permission denied — grant in System Settings → Privacy & Security → Accessibility");
 269:         all_ok = false;
 270:     }
 271: 
 272:     // 4. Repo connectivity (optional)
 273:     if let Some(repo) = &args.repo {
 274:         println!("  → cloning '{}' to verify network + git…", repo);
 275:         let dummy_cfg = config::Config::default();
 276:         match repo::fetch(repo, &dummy_cfg).await {
 277:             Ok(p) => println!("  ✓ repo cloned to {}", p.display()),
 278:             Err(e) => {
 279:                 println!("  ✗ repo fetch failed: {e}");
 280:                 all_ok = false;
 281:             }
 282:         }
 283:     }
 284: 
 285:     // 5. API key (optional)
 286:     if args.api {
 287:         let key = config::resolve_api_key(args.api_key.as_deref());
 288:         if key.is_empty() {
 289:             println!("  ✗ ANTHROPIC_API_KEY not set — set it in your shell or pass --api-key");
 290:             all_ok = false;
 291:         } else {
 292:             print!("  → pinging Anthropic API ({} chars)… ", key.len());
 293:             match orchestrator::llm::ping_api(&key).await {
 294:                 Ok(()) => println!("✓ valid"),
 295:                 Err(e) => {
 296:                     println!("✗ rejected — {e}");
 297:                     all_ok = false;
 298:                 }
 299:             }
 300:         }
 301:     }
 302: 
 303:     if all_ok {
 304:         println!("\nAll checks passed.");
 305:     } else {
 306:         bail!("One or more checks failed — fix the issues above and re-run.");
 307:     }
 308: 
 309:     Ok(())
 310: }
 311: 
 312: // ─── ghostwriter plan ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 313: 
 314: async fn cmd_plan(args: cli::PlanArgs) -> Result<()> {
 315:     let dummy_run_args = cli::RunArgs {
 316:         repo: args.repo.clone(),
 317:         dest: std::path::PathBuf::from("."),
 318:         config: args.config.clone(),
 319:         skip: args.skip.clone(),
 320:         wpm: None,
 321:         speed: 1.0,
 322:         no_typos: false,
 323:         seed: None,
 324:         api_key: args.api_key.clone(),
 325:         script: None,
 326:         no_cache: args.no_cache,
 327:         from_stage: None,
 328:         stages: Vec::new(),
 329:         pause_between_stages: false,
 330:         no_bootstrap: true,
 331:         strict: false,
 332:         overwrite: false,
 333:         dry_run: false,
 334:         from_commits: false,
 335:         no_commits: true, // plan always uses AI; --from-commits not applicable here
 336:     };
 337: 
 338:     let cfg = config::Config::load_or_default(&dummy_run_args)?;
 339:     let api_key = config::resolve_api_key(args.api_key.as_deref());
 340: 
 341:     let source_root = repo::fetch(&args.repo, &cfg).await?;
 342:     let cache_path = if args.no_cache {
 343:         None
 344:     } else {
 345:         Some(source_root.join(".ghostwriter").join("script.json"))
 346:     };
 347:     let script = orchestrator::orchestrate(&source_root, cache_path.as_deref(), &cfg, &api_key)
 348:         .await?;
 349: 
 350:     let json = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&script)?;
 351: 
 352:     if let Some(save_path) = &args.save {
 353:         std::fs::write(save_path, &json)?;
 354:         println!("Script written to {}", save_path.display());
 355:     } else {
 356:         println!("{json}");
 357:     }
 358: 
 359:     if args.verbose {
 360:         eprintln!("\n--- Stage summary ---");
 361:         for (i, stage) in script.stages.iter().enumerate() {
 362:             eprintln!("  {:2}. {} — {} actions", i + 1, stage.name, stage.actions.len());
 363:         }
 364:     }
 365: 
 366:     Ok(())
 367: }
 368: 
 369: // ─── Orchestration mode selection ────────────────────────────────────────────
 370: 
 371: enum OrchestrationMode {
 372:     Commits,
 373:     Ai,
 374: }
 375: 
 376: /// Single decision point for commit-mode vs AI-mode.
 377: ///
 378: /// Order of precedence:
 379: ///   1. `--from-commits` → always commit mode
 380: ///   2. `--no-commits`   → always AI mode
 381: ///   3. auto: use commit mode if `assess_history` says history is usable
 382: fn pick_orchestration_mode(args: &cli::RunArgs, source_root: &std::path::Path) -> OrchestrationMode {
 383:     if args.from_commits {
 384:         info!("orchestration mode: commit-based (--from-commits)");
 385:         return OrchestrationMode::Commits;
 386:     }
 387:     if args.no_commits {
 388:         info!("orchestration mode: AI (--no-commits)");
 389:         return OrchestrationMode::Ai;
 390:     }
 391:     match repo::history::assess_history(source_root) {
 392:         Ok(q) if q.is_usable => {
 393:             info!("orchestration mode: commit-based (auto: {})", q.reason);
 394:             OrchestrationMode::Commits
 395:         }
 396:         Ok(q) => {
 397:             info!("orchestration mode: AI (auto: {})", q.reason);
 398:             OrchestrationMode::Ai
 399:         }
 400:         Err(e) => {
 401:             tracing::warn!("history assessment failed ({e}), falling back to AI mode");
 402:             OrchestrationMode::Ai
 403:         }
 404:     }
 405: }
 406: 
 407: // ─── Shared stage runner ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 408: 
 409: /// Iterate over stages, skip already-completed ones, checkpoint after each.
 410: async fn run_stages(
 411:     script: &orchestrator::script::DevelopmentScript,
 412:     dest: &std::path::Path,
 413:     cfg: &config::Config,
 414:     seed: Option<u64>,
 415:     session: &mut session::SessionState,
 416:     from_stage: Option<&str>,
 417:     all_stage_names: &[String],
 418:     pause_between_stages: bool,
 419:     nav: &mut navigator::Navigator,
 420: ) -> Result<()> {
 421:     let mut reached_start = from_stage.is_none();
 422: 
 423:     let bar = ProgressBar::new(script.stages.len() as u64);
 424:     bar.set_style(
 425:         ProgressStyle::default_bar()
 426:             .template("[{elapsed_precise}] {bar:40} {pos}/{len} {msg}")?
 427:             .progress_chars("=> "),
 428:     );
 429: 
 430:     for stage in &script.stages {
 431:         // Fast-forward to the requested start stage
 432:         if !reached_start {
 433:             if from_stage == Some(stage.name.as_str()) {
 434:                 reached_start = true;
 435:             } else {
 436:                 info!("skipping stage '{}' (before --from-stage)", stage.name);
 437:                 bar.inc(1);
 438:                 continue;
 439:             }
 440:         }
 441: 
 442:         // Skip already-completed stages (checkpoint recovery)
 443:         if session.is_stage_done(&stage.name) {
 444:             info!("skipping stage '{}' (already completed)", stage.name);
 445:             bar.inc(1);
 446:             continue;
 447:         }
 448: 
 449:         bar.set_message(stage.name.clone());
 450:         info!("running stage '{}'", stage.name);
 451:         typer::engine::run_stage(stage, dest, cfg, seed, nav).await?;
 452: 
 453:         // Persist checkpoint immediately after stage completes
 454:         session.complete_stage(&stage.name, all_stage_names)?;
 455:         info!("checkpoint saved after stage '{}'", stage.name);
 456:         bar.inc(1);
 457: 
 458:         if pause_between_stages {
 459:             eprintln!("  [stage '{}' complete — press Enter to continue]", stage.name);
 460:             let mut _buf = String::new();
 461:             let _ = std::io::stdin().read_line(&mut _buf);
 462:         }
 463:     }
 464: 
 465:     bar.finish_with_message("done");
 466:     Ok(())
 467: }

===== /src/navigator/bootstrap.rs =====

   1: //! Bootstrap — the one-time setup that must complete before any playback begins.
   2: //!
   3: //! The 9-step sequence runs on the COMPLETE source repo so every file template
   4: //! is captured before any playback writing begins.
   5: //!
   6: //!  1.  Pre-flight checks (VSCode CLI, screen capture, input injection).
   7: //!  2.  Open VSCode with the source repo and wait for it to become visible.
   8: //!  3.  Force fullscreen so sidebar dimensions are fixed for this session.
   9: //!  4.  Detect the DPI scale factor (physical px ↔ logical px).
  10: //!  5.  Ensure the Explorer sidebar is open and showing the file tree.
  11: //!  6.  Detect sidebar bounds (activity bar → sidebar right edge).
  12: //!  7.  Expand every folder in the tree.
  13: //!  8.  Open each file, capture its selected-row template, store in dictionary.
  14: //!  9.  Verify a random sample of templates (NCC score must exceed threshold).
  15: //!
  16: //! The dictionary is keyed by RELATIVE paths so the same templates work when
  17: //! playback writes into a different directory (`dest`) from the source repo.
  18: 
  19: use crate::navigator::{
  20:     bootstrap_error::BootstrapError,
  21:     dictionary::VisualDictionary,
  22:     mouse,
  23:     screen::{self, Rect},
  24:     sidebar::{crop_row, find_selected_row},
  25:     template,
  26: };
  27: use crate::typer::vscode;
  28: use enigo::{Enigo, Key, Keyboard, Settings};
  29: use image::{DynamicImage, GenericImageView};
  30: use indicatif::{ProgressBar, ProgressStyle};
  31: use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
  32: use tokio::time::{sleep, Duration};
  33: use tracing::{info, warn};
  34: 
  35: // ─── Public output ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  36: 
  37: #[derive(Debug)]
  38: pub struct BootstrapResult {
  39:     pub dictionary: VisualDictionary,
  40:     pub layout: VscodeLayout,
  41: }
  42: 
  43: /// Stable layout measurements captured during bootstrap.
  44: /// All values are in LOGICAL pixels (after DPI correction).
  45: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]
  46: #[allow(dead_code)]
  47: pub struct VscodeLayout {
  48:     /// Physical pixels per logical pixel (1.0 on standard DPI, 2.0 on Retina).
  49:     pub scale_factor: f32,
  50:     pub display_width: u32,
  51:     pub display_height: u32,
  52:     pub sidebar: Rect,
  53:     pub row_height_px: u32,
  54: }
  55: 
  56: /// Logical x-centre of the VSCode activity bar (hardcoded VSCode constant).
  57: const ACTIVITY_BAR_CENTRE_X: i32 = 24;
  58: /// Logical y of the Explorer icon (always the first icon, ~40px from top).
  59: const ACTIVITY_BAR_EXPLORER_Y: i32 = 40;
  60: /// Logical width of the activity bar.
  61: pub const ACTIVITY_BAR_WIDTH: u32 = 48;
  62: /// Minimum fraction of files that must have captured templates before we proceed.
  63: const MIN_TEMPLATE_PCT: u8 = 80;
  64: /// Maximum number of verify-sample failures allowed before we reject the dictionary.
  65: const MAX_VERIFY_FAILURES: u32 = 2;
  66: /// How long to wait for VSCode to appear on screen after launch.
  67: const VSCODE_LAUNCH_TIMEOUT_MS: u64 = 6_000;
  68: /// Poll interval while waiting for VSCode to appear.
  69: const VSCODE_POLL_INTERVAL_MS: u64 = 500;
  70: 
  71: // ─── Entry point ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  72: 
  73: /// Run the full 9-step bootstrap sequence.
  74: ///
  75: /// `source_root`: path to the COMPLETE, already-cloned repo.
  76: /// `dest`:        where playback will write — used only for dictionary storage.
  77: /// `file_paths`:  relative paths of all files the script will touch.
  78: pub async fn run(
  79:     source_root: &Path,
  80:     dest: &Path,
  81:     file_paths: &[PathBuf],
  82: ) -> Result<BootstrapResult, BootstrapError> {
  83:     // Ensure the diagnostics directory exists before anything else so every
  84:     // failure handler can write screenshots there.
  85:     let diag_dir = dest.join(".ghostwriter").join("diagnostics");
  86:     std::fs::create_dir_all(&diag_dir).map_err(|e| BootstrapError::Io {
  87:         step: "setup diagnostics dir".to_string(),
  88:         source: e,
  89:     })?;
  90: 
  91:     // ── Cache hit ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  92:     if let Some(dict) = VisualDictionary::load(dest).map_err(|e| io_err("load cached dictionary", e))? {
  93:         info!("bootstrap: cached dictionary found ({} templates)", dict.len());
  94:         let scale = screen::scale_factor().map_err(|e| io_err("read scale factor", e))?;
  95:         let physical_bounds = screen::primary_display_bounds().map_err(|e| io_err("read display bounds", e))?;
  96:         let logical_w = (physical_bounds.width as f32 / scale) as u32;
  97:         let logical_h = (physical_bounds.height as f32 / scale) as u32;
  98:         let layout = VscodeLayout {
  99:             scale_factor: scale,
 100:             display_width: logical_w,
 101:             display_height: logical_h,
 102:             sidebar: dict.sidebar_region,
 103:             row_height_px: dict.row_height_px,
 104:         };
 105:         return Ok(BootstrapResult { dictionary: dict, layout });
 106:     }
 107: 
 108:     info!(
 109:         "bootstrap: starting 9-step sequence on source repo {}",
 110:         source_root.display()
 111:     );
 112: 
 113:     // ── Step 1: Pre-flight checks ────────────────────────────────────────────
 114:     info!("bootstrap: {}", crate::navigator::bootstrap_error::STEPS[0]);
 115:     preflight_check(&diag_dir).await?;
 116: 
 117:     // ── Step 2: Open VSCode ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
 118:     info!("bootstrap: {}", crate::navigator::bootstrap_error::STEPS[1]);
 119:     open_vscode_and_wait(source_root, &diag_dir).await?;
 120: 
 121:     // ── Step 3: Force fullscreen ─────────────────────────────────────────────
 122:     info!("bootstrap: {}", crate::navigator::bootstrap_error::STEPS[2]);
 123:     force_fullscreen(&diag_dir).await?;
 124:     sleep(Duration::from_millis(1200)).await;
 125: 
 126:     // ── Step 4: DPI + display bounds ─────────────────────────────────────────
 127:     info!("bootstrap: {}", crate::navigator::bootstrap_error::STEPS[3]);
 128:     let scale = screen::scale_factor().map_err(|e| io_err("4/9 detect DPI scale", e))?;
 129:     info!("bootstrap: DPI scale factor = {scale}");
 130:     let physical_bounds = screen::primary_display_bounds().map_err(|e| io_err("4/9 detect DPI scale", e))?;
 131:     let logical_w = (physical_bounds.width as f32 / scale) as u32;
 132:     let logical_h = (physical_bounds.height as f32 / scale) as u32;
 133:     info!("bootstrap: logical display = {logical_w}×{logical_h}");
 134: 
 135:     // ── Step 5 + 6: Open Explorer sidebar and detect its bounds ─────────────
 136:     info!("bootstrap: {}", crate::navigator::bootstrap_error::STEPS[4]);
 137:     let sidebar_rect = ensure_explorer_open(scale, &diag_dir).await?;
 138:     info!(
 139:         "bootstrap: sidebar = x:{} y:{} w:{} h:{}",
 140:         sidebar_rect.x, sidebar_rect.y, sidebar_rect.width, sidebar_rect.height
 141:     );
 142: 
 143:     // ── Step 7: Expand all folders ───────────────────────────────────────────
 144:     info!("bootstrap: {}", crate::navigator::bootstrap_error::STEPS[6]);
 145:     expand_all_folders(&diag_dir).await?;
 146:     sleep(Duration::from_millis(1500)).await;
 147:     info!("bootstrap: file tree expanded");
 148: 
 149:     // ── Step 8: Capture templates ─────────────────────────────────────────────
 150:     info!("bootstrap: {}", crate::navigator::bootstrap_error::STEPS[7]);
 151:     let mut dict = VisualDictionary::new(dest, sidebar_rect);
 152:     let mut y_samples: Vec<u32> = Vec::new();
 153:     let mut failed_files: Vec<PathBuf> = Vec::new();
 154: 
 155:     let bar = ProgressBar::new(file_paths.len() as u64);
 156:     bar.set_style(
 157:         ProgressStyle::default_bar()
 158:             .template("[bootstrap] {bar:35} {pos}/{len}  {msg}")
 159:             .unwrap_or_else(|_| ProgressStyle::default_bar())
 160:             .progress_chars("=> "),
 161:     );
 162: 
 163:     for rel_path in file_paths {
 164:         bar.set_message(rel_path.to_string_lossy().to_string());
 165:         let abs = source_root.join(rel_path);
 166: 
 167:         if !abs.exists() {
 168:             warn!("bootstrap: skipping non-existent file {}", rel_path.display());
 169:             failed_files.push(rel_path.clone());
 170:             bar.inc(1);
 171:             continue;
 172:         }
 173: 
 174:         match capture_template(&abs, &dict, scale).await {
 175:             Ok((tmpl, screen_y)) => {
 176:                 dict.insert(rel_path, tmpl, Some(screen_y))
 177:                     .map_err(|e| io_err("8/9 capture file templates", e))?;
 178:                 y_samples.push(screen_y);
 179:             }
 180:             Err(e) => {
 181:                 warn!("bootstrap: could not capture {}: {e}", rel_path.display());
 182:                 failed_files.push(rel_path.clone());
 183:                 save_diagnostic_screenshot(
 184:                     &diag_dir,
 185:                     &format!("template_fail_{}", sanitize_filename(rel_path)),
 186:                 );
 187:             }
 188:         }
 189: 
 190:         bar.inc(1);
 191:     }
 192: 
 193:     bar.finish_with_message("templates captured");
 194: 
 195:     // Enforce minimum coverage threshold.
 196:     let captured = file_paths.len() - failed_files.len();
 197:     let total = file_paths.len();
 198:     let min_required = ((total as f32 * MIN_TEMPLATE_PCT as f32 / 100.0).ceil()) as usize;
 199: 
 200:     if captured < min_required {
 201:         let failed_list = failed_files
 202:             .iter()
 203:             .map(|p| format!("  • {}", p.display()))
 204:             .collect::<Vec<_>>()
 205:             .join("\n");
 206:         return Err(BootstrapError::TooFewTemplates {
 207:             captured,
 208:             total,
 209:             min_required,
 210:             min_pct: MIN_TEMPLATE_PCT,
 211:             failed_list,
 212:             diag_dir,
 213:         });
 214:     }
 215: 
 216:     // ── Row height measurement ────────────────────────────────────────────────
 217:     dict.row_height_px = measure_row_height(&y_samples);
 218:     info!("bootstrap: row height = {}px", dict.row_height_px);
 219: 
 220:     // Capture parent directory rows (best-effort, uses measured row height).
 221:     capture_parent_dir_templates(file_paths, source_root, &mut dict, scale).await;
 222: 
 223:     // ── Step 9: Verify sample ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
 224:     info!("bootstrap: {}", crate::navigator::bootstrap_error::STEPS[8]);
 225:     verify_sample(&dict, file_paths, source_root, scale, &diag_dir).await?;
 226: 
 227:     // ── Save dictionary ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 228:     let dict_path = dest.join(".ghostwriter").join("dictionary.json");
 229:     dict.save().map_err(|e| BootstrapError::DictionarySaveFailed {
 230:         path: dict_path,
 231:         reason: e.to_string(),
 232:     })?;
 233:     info!("bootstrap: dictionary saved ({} templates)", dict.len());
 234: 
 235:     let layout = VscodeLayout {
 236:         scale_factor: scale,
 237:         display_width: logical_w,
 238:         display_height: logical_h,
 239:         sidebar: sidebar_rect,
 240:         row_height_px: dict.row_height_px,
 241:     };
 242: 
 243:     Ok(BootstrapResult { dictionary: dict, layout })
 244: }
 245: 
 246: // ─── Step 1: Pre-flight checks ────────────────────────────────────────────────
 247: 
 248: async fn preflight_check(diag_dir: &Path) -> Result<(), BootstrapError> {
 249:     // 1a. VSCode CLI must be on PATH.
 250:     let code_on_path = std::process::Command::new("code")
 251:         .arg("--version")
 252:         .output()
 253:         .map(|o| o.status.success())
 254:         .unwrap_or(false);
 255: 
 256:     if !code_on_path {
 257:         return Err(BootstrapError::VscodeCliMissing);
 258:     }
 259: 
 260:     // 1b. Screen capture permission — take a screenshot and check for
 261:     //     a fully black image (indicates OS denied capture permission).
 262:     let screenshot = screen::capture_screen()
 263:         .map_err(|e| io_err("1/9 pre-flight checks", e))?;
 264: 
 265:     if is_all_black(&screenshot) {
 266:         save_diagnostic_screenshot(diag_dir, "preflight_black_screen");
 267:         return Err(BootstrapError::ScreenCapturePermissionDenied);
 268:     }
 269: 
 270:     // 1b-linux. wmctrl must be installed for window focus on Linux.
 271:     #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
 272:     {
 273:         let wmctrl_ok = std::process::Command::new("which")
 274:             .arg("wmctrl")
 275:             .output()
 276:             .map(|o| o.status.success())
 277:             .unwrap_or(false);
 278:         if !wmctrl_ok {
 279:             return Err(BootstrapError::WmctrlMissing);
 280:         }
 281:     }
 282: 
 283:     // 1c. Input injection — attempt to press+release Shift via Enigo.
 284:     //     This is the minimal gesture that fails if accessibility is blocked.
 285:     let injection_ok = tokio::task::spawn_blocking(|| {
 286:         let mut e = match Enigo::new(&Settings::default()) {
 287:             Ok(e) => e,
 288:             Err(_) => return false,
 289:         };
 290:         e.key(Key::Shift, enigo::Direction::Press).is_ok()
 291:             && e.key(Key::Shift, enigo::Direction::Release).is_ok()
 292:     })
 293:     .await
 294:     .unwrap_or(false);
 295: 
 296:     if !injection_ok {
 297:         return Err(BootstrapError::InputInjectionPermissionDenied);
 298:     }
 299: 
 300:     info!("bootstrap: pre-flight checks passed");
 301:     Ok(())
 302: }
 303: 
 304: /// Returns `true` if the image appears to be entirely black (screen capture blocked).
 305: fn is_all_black(img: &DynamicImage) -> bool {
 306:     let (w, h) = (img.width(), img.height());
 307:     // Sample a 3×3 grid spread across the image.
 308:     let points = [
 309:         (w / 4, h / 4),
 310:         (w / 2, h / 4),
 311:         (3 * w / 4, h / 4),
 312:         (w / 4, h / 2),
 313:         (w / 2, h / 2),
 314:         (3 * w / 4, h / 2),
 315:         (w / 4, 3 * h / 4),
 316:         (w / 2, 3 * h / 4),
 317:         (3 * w / 4, 3 * h / 4),
 318:     ];
 319:     points
 320:         .iter()
 321:         .all(|&(x, y)| pixel_luma(img, x, y) < 8)
 322: }
 323: 
 324: // ─── Step 2: Open VSCode ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 325: 
 326: /// Open VSCode with the source repo and poll until it becomes visible on screen.
 327: ///
 328: /// Visibility is determined by looking for the characteristic dark activity-bar
 329: /// column at the left edge of the screen.
 330: async fn open_vscode_and_wait(
 331:     source_root: &Path,
 332:     diag_dir: &Path,
 333: ) -> Result<(), BootstrapError> {
 334:     vscode::open(source_root)
 335:         .map_err(|e| io_err("2/9 open VSCode", e))?;
 336: 
 337:     let start = std::time::Instant::now();
 338:     loop {
 339:         sleep(Duration::from_millis(VSCODE_POLL_INTERVAL_MS)).await;
 340: 
 341:         if let Ok(screenshot) = screen::capture_screen() {
 342:             let scale = screen::scale_factor().unwrap_or(1.0);
 343:             let logical = scale_down(&screenshot, scale);
 344:             if looks_like_vscode_activity_bar(&logical) {
 345:                 info!(
 346:                     "bootstrap: VSCode detected after {}ms",
 347:                     start.elapsed().as_millis()
 348:                 );
 349:                 return Ok(());
 350:             }
 351:         }
 352: 
 353:         if start.elapsed().as_millis() as u64 >= VSCODE_LAUNCH_TIMEOUT_MS {
 354:             let diag_path = save_diagnostic_screenshot(diag_dir, "vscode_not_visible");
 355:             return Err(BootstrapError::VscodeNotVisible {
 356:                 waited_ms: VSCODE_LAUNCH_TIMEOUT_MS,
 357:                 path: source_root.to_path_buf(),
 358:                 diag_path,
 359:             });
 360:         }
 361:     }
 362: }
 363: 
 364: /// Heuristic: is the VSCode activity bar visible on the left side of the screen?
 365: ///
 366: /// The activity bar is a narrow, consistently-coloured strip.  On dark themes
 367: /// it's dark; on light themes it's pale.  Either way it differs clearly from
 368: /// the editor area to its right.  We compare the mean luma of the first 48px
 369: /// against the centre column — a difference of > 15 luma units is our signal.
 370: fn looks_like_vscode_activity_bar(img: &DynamicImage) -> bool {
 371:     let ab_x = ACTIVITY_BAR_CENTRE_X as u32;
 372:     let mid_x = img.width() / 2;
 373:     if ab_x >= img.width() || mid_x >= img.width() {
 374:         return false;
 375:     }
 376: 
 377:     let (mut ab_sum, mut mid_sum, mut count) = (0u32, 0u32, 0u32);
 378:     for y in (10u32..img.height().min(400)).step_by(20) {
 379:         ab_sum += pixel_luma(img, ab_x, y) as u32;
 380:         mid_sum += pixel_luma(img, mid_x, y) as u32;
 381:         count += 1;
 382:     }
 383:     if count == 0 {
 384:         return false;
 385:     }
 386:     let ab_mean = (ab_sum / count) as i32;
 387:     let mid_mean = (mid_sum / count) as i32;
 388: 
 389:     // Activity bar should differ from the editor by at least 15 luma units
 390:     // (it can be darker OR lighter depending on theme).
 391:     (ab_mean - mid_mean).abs() > 15
 392: }
 393: 
 394: // ─── Step 3: Force fullscreen ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
 395: 
 396: /// Press the platform fullscreen shortcut and verify the result.
 397: ///
 398: /// Retries up to 2 times.  If after both attempts the estimated window area is
 399: /// still less than 90% of the display, returns `BootstrapError::FullscreenFailed`.
 400: async fn force_fullscreen(diag_dir: &Path) -> Result<(), BootstrapError> {
 401:     vscode::focus()
 402:         .map_err(|e| io_err("3/9 force fullscreen", e))?;
 403:     sleep(Duration::from_millis(300)).await;
 404: 
 405:     let display = screen::primary_display_bounds()
 406:         .map_err(|e| io_err("3/9 force fullscreen", e))?;
 407:     let scale = screen::scale_factor().unwrap_or(1.0);
 408:     let display_area = ((display.width as f32 / scale) as u32)
 409:         * ((display.height as f32 / scale) as u32);
 410: 
 411:     for attempt in 0..2u8 {
 412:         toggle_fullscreen_shortcut();
 413:         sleep(Duration::from_millis(1200)).await;
 414: 
 415:         let screenshot = screen::capture_screen()
 416:             .map_err(|e| io_err("3/9 force fullscreen", e))?;
 417:         let logical = scale_down(&screenshot, scale);
 418: 
 419:         if is_fullscreen(&logical) {
 420:             info!("bootstrap: fullscreen confirmed (attempt {})", attempt + 1);
 421:             return Ok(());
 422:         }
 423:         info!("bootstrap: fullscreen not confirmed on attempt {}, retrying…", attempt + 1);
 424:     }
 425: 
 426:     // Both attempts failed — capture a diagnostic and report.
 427:     let screenshot = screen::capture_screen()
 428:         .map_err(|e| io_err("3/9 force fullscreen", e))?;
 429:     let logical = scale_down(&screenshot, scale);
 430:     let window_area = logical.width() * logical.height().saturating_sub(40); // subtract titlebar estimate
 431:     let diag_path = save_diagnostic_screenshot(diag_dir, "fullscreen_failed");
 432: 
 433:     Err(BootstrapError::FullscreenFailed {
 434:         window_area,
 435:         display_area,
 436:         diag_path,
 437:     })
 438: }
 439: 
 440: fn toggle_fullscreen_shortcut() {
 441:     let Ok(mut enigo) = Enigo::new(&Settings::default()) else { return };
 442: 
 443:     #[cfg(not(target_os = "macos"))]
 444:     { enigo.key(Key::F11, enigo::Direction::Click).ok(); }
 445: 
 446:     #[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
 447:     {
 448:         enigo.key(Key::Control, enigo::Direction::Press).ok();
 449:         enigo.key(Key::Meta, enigo::Direction::Press).ok();
 450:         enigo.key(Key::Unicode('f'), enigo::Direction::Click).ok();
 451:         enigo.key(Key::Meta, enigo::Direction::Release).ok();
 452:         enigo.key(Key::Control, enigo::Direction::Release).ok();
 453:     }
 454: }
 455: 
 456: /// Heuristic: is VSCode in fullscreen?
 457: ///
 458: /// When not fullscreen, macOS shows traffic-light buttons at the very top of
 459: /// the window (y ≈ 0–30px are bright / grey).  When fullscreen, the VSCode
 460: /// theme colour starts from y=0.  We check whether the top-left strip is
 461: /// similarly dark to the activity bar column.
 462: fn is_fullscreen(logical_img: &DynamicImage) -> bool {
 463:     let ab_x = ACTIVITY_BAR_CENTRE_X as u32;
 464:     if ab_x >= logical_img.width() || logical_img.height() < 50 {
 465:         return true; // can't tell; be optimistic
 466:     }
 467: 
 468:     let top_luma = pixel_luma(logical_img, ab_x, 5);
 469:     let body_luma = pixel_luma(logical_img, ab_x, 50);
 470: 
 471:     // In fullscreen the two samples should be similar (both VSCode background).
 472:     // If there's a big difference, a bright titlebar is present at the top.
 473:     (top_luma as i32 - body_luma as i32).abs() < 35
 474: }
 475: 
 476: // ─── Step 5 + 6: Ensure Explorer open + sidebar bounds ───────────────────────
 477: 
 478: const EXPLORER_SHORTCUT_ATTEMPTS: u8 = 3;
 479: 
 480: async fn ensure_explorer_open(scale: f32, diag_dir: &Path) -> Result<Rect, BootstrapError> {
 481:     for attempt in 0..EXPLORER_SHORTCUT_ATTEMPTS {
 482:         press_explorer_shortcut()
 483:             .map_err(|e| io_err("5/9 open Explorer sidebar", e))?;
 484:         sleep(Duration::from_millis(600)).await;
 485: 
 486:         let screenshot = screen::capture_screen()
 487:             .map_err(|e| io_err("5/9 open Explorer sidebar", e))?;
 488:         let logical = scale_down(&screenshot, scale);
 489: 
 490:         match probe_sidebar(&logical) {
 491:             SidebarProbe::Found(rect) => return Ok(rect),
 492: 
 493:             // Sidebar boundary found but no file tree rows — structural error,
 494:             // no point retrying.
 495:             SidebarProbe::EmptySidebar { x, width } => {
 496:                 let diag_path = save_diagnostic_screenshot(diag_dir, "sidebar_empty");
 497:                 return Err(BootstrapError::SidebarEmpty { x, width, diag_path });
 498:             }
 499: 
 500:             // No boundary detected yet — try activity bar click on first miss.
 501:             SidebarProbe::NotFound => {
 502:                 if attempt == 0 {
 503:                     info!(
 504:                         "bootstrap: shortcut didn't open sidebar, \
 505:                          clicking activity bar Explorer icon"
 506:                     );
 507:                     mouse::click(ACTIVITY_BAR_CENTRE_X, ACTIVITY_BAR_EXPLORER_Y)
 508:                         .await
 509:                         .map_err(|e| io_err("5/9 open Explorer sidebar", e))?;
 510:                     sleep(Duration::from_millis(700)).await;
 511:                 }
 512:             }
 513:         }
 514:     }
 515: 
 516:     // All attempts exhausted — take a final screenshot to decide on the error.
 517:     let screenshot = screen::capture_screen()
 518:         .map_err(|e| io_err("5/9 open Explorer sidebar", e))?;
 519:     let logical = scale_down(&screenshot, scale);
 520:     let scan_width = (logical.width() / 2).min(600);
 521:     let diag_path = save_diagnostic_screenshot(diag_dir, "explorer_not_open");
 522: 
 523:     // If we can detect a boundary but still can't validate a sidebar, report
 524:     // that the bounds couldn't be measured (theme / narrow sidebar issue).
 525:     if find_sidebar_right_edge(&logical).is_some() {
 526:         Err(BootstrapError::SidebarBoundsNotFound { scan_width, diag_path })
 527:     } else {
 528:         Err(BootstrapError::ExplorerNotOpen {
 529:             attempts: EXPLORER_SHORTCUT_ATTEMPTS,
 530:             diag_path,
 531:         })
 532:     }
 533: }
 534: 
 535: /// Result of probing a screenshot for a VSCode Explorer sidebar.
 536: enum SidebarProbe {
 537:     /// A valid sidebar with file tree rows was found.
 538:     Found(Rect),
 539:     /// A sidebar boundary was found but the region has no file tree content.
 540:     EmptySidebar { x: u32, width: u32 },
 541:     /// No sidebar boundary detectable at all.
 542:     NotFound,
 543: }
 544: 
 545: fn probe_sidebar(img: &DynamicImage) -> SidebarProbe {
 546:     let right_edge = match find_sidebar_right_edge(img) {
 547:         Some(x) => x,
 548:         None => return SidebarProbe::NotFound,
 549:     };
 550: 
 551:     let sidebar_width = right_edge.saturating_sub(ACTIVITY_BAR_WIDTH);
 552:     if sidebar_width < 80 {
 553:         return SidebarProbe::NotFound; // boundary pixel noise, not a real sidebar
 554:     }
 555: 
 556:     let candidate = Rect::new(ACTIVITY_BAR_WIDTH, 0, sidebar_width, img.height());
 557: 
 558:     if has_file_tree_structure(img, candidate) {
 559:         SidebarProbe::Found(candidate)
 560:     } else {
 561:         SidebarProbe::EmptySidebar {
 562:             x: candidate.x,
 563:             width: candidate.width,
 564:         }
 565:     }
 566: }
 567: 
 568: fn press_explorer_shortcut() -> anyhow::Result<()> {
 569:     vscode::focus()?;
 570:     std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(150));
 571: 
 572:     let mut enigo = Enigo::new(&Settings::default())
 573:         .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!("{e:?}"))?;
 574: 
 575:     #[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
 576:     let modifier = Key::Meta;
 577:     #[cfg(not(target_os = "macos"))]
 578:     let modifier = Key::Control;
 579: 
 580:     enigo.key(modifier, enigo::Direction::Press).ok();
 581:     enigo.key(Key::Shift, enigo::Direction::Press).ok();
 582:     enigo.key(Key::Unicode('e'), enigo::Direction::Click).ok();
 583:     enigo.key(Key::Shift, enigo::Direction::Release).ok();
 584:     enigo.key(modifier, enigo::Direction::Release).ok();
 585:     Ok(())
 586: }
 587: 
 588: // ─── Step 7: Expand all folders ──────────────────────────────────────────────
 589: 
 590: /// Open the Command Palette and run "Explorer: Expand All".
 591: ///
 592: /// After sending the command we verify that the palette dismissed (i.e. the
 593: /// Return key was accepted).  If the palette appears stuck, we report
 594: /// `ExpandAllFailed`.
 595: async fn expand_all_folders(diag_dir: &Path) -> Result<(), BootstrapError> {
 596:     // Open Command Palette: Ctrl/Cmd + Shift + P
 597:     let palette_opened = tokio::task::spawn_blocking(|| -> bool {
 598:         let Ok(mut enigo) = Enigo::new(&Settings::default()) else { return false };
 599: 
 600:         #[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
 601:         let modifier = Key::Meta;
 602:         #[cfg(not(target_os = "macos"))]
 603:         let modifier = Key::Control;
 604: 
 605:         enigo.key(modifier, enigo::Direction::Press).ok();
 606:         enigo.key(Key::Shift, enigo::Direction::Press).ok();
 607:         enigo.key(Key::Unicode('p'), enigo::Direction::Click).ok();
 608:         enigo.key(Key::Shift, enigo::Direction::Release).ok();
 609:         enigo.key(modifier, enigo::Direction::Release).ok();
 610:         true
 611:     })
 612:     .await
 613:     .unwrap_or(false);
 614: 
 615:     if !palette_opened {
 616:         let diag_path = save_diagnostic_screenshot(diag_dir, "expand_palette_open_failed");
 617:         return Err(BootstrapError::ExpandAllFailed { diag_path });
 618:     }
 619: 
 620:     sleep(Duration::from_millis(500)).await;
 621: 
 622:     // Type the command name and press Enter.
 623:     let text_typed = tokio::task::spawn_blocking(|| -> bool {
 624:         let Ok(mut enigo) = Enigo::new(&Settings::default()) else { return false };
 625:         enigo.text("Explorer: Expand All").is_ok()
 626:     })
 627:     .await
 628:     .unwrap_or(false);
 629: 
 630:     if !text_typed {
 631:         let diag_path = save_diagnostic_screenshot(diag_dir, "expand_type_failed");
 632:         return Err(BootstrapError::ExpandAllFailed { diag_path });
 633:     }
 634: 
 635:     sleep(Duration::from_millis(300)).await;
 636: 
 637:     // Press Enter to confirm the selected command.
 638:     let _ = tokio::task::spawn_blocking(|| -> bool {
 639:         let Ok(mut enigo) = Enigo::new(&Settings::default()) else { return false };
 640:         enigo.key(Key::Return, enigo::Direction::Click).is_ok()
 641:     })
 642:     .await
 643:     .unwrap_or(false);
 644: 
 645:     // Allow time for the command to execute and the palette to dismiss.
 646:     sleep(Duration::from_millis(800)).await;
 647: 
 648:     // Verify: the command palette should now be gone.
 649:     let screenshot = screen::capture_screen()
 650:         .map_err(|e| io_err("7/9 expand file tree", e))?;
 651:     let scale = screen::scale_factor().unwrap_or(1.0);
 652:     let logical = scale_down(&screenshot, scale);
 653: 
 654:     if looks_like_command_palette_open(&logical) {
 655:         let diag_path =
 656:             save_diagnostic_screenshot(diag_dir, "expand_all_palette_stuck");
 657:         return Err(BootstrapError::ExpandAllFailed { diag_path });
 658:     }
 659: 
 660:     Ok(())
 661: }
 662: 
 663: /// Heuristic: does the screenshot look like the Command Palette is still open?
 664: ///
 665: /// The palette renders as a bright floating box near the top-centre.  We sample
 666: /// luma at y=60, centre-x vs sidebar-x — palette input is brighter than both.
 667: fn looks_like_command_palette_open(img: &DynamicImage) -> bool {
 668:     if img.height() < 80 {
 669:         return false;
 670:     }
 671:     let cx = img.width() / 2;
 672:     let top_luma = pixel_luma(img, cx, 60) as i32;
 673:     let sb_luma = pixel_luma(img, ACTIVITY_BAR_WIDTH + 20, 60) as i32;
 674:     // Palette input box is significantly brighter than sidebar background.
 675:     (top_luma - sb_luma) > 40
 676: }
 677: 
 678: // ─── Step 9: Verify sample ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 679: 
 680: async fn verify_sample(
 681:     dict: &VisualDictionary,
 682:     file_paths: &[PathBuf],
 683:     source_root: &Path,
 684:     scale: f32,
 685:     diag_dir: &Path,
 686: ) -> Result<(), BootstrapError> {
 687:     let sample: Vec<&PathBuf> = {
 688:         use rand::seq::SliceRandom;
 689:         let mut rng = rand::thread_rng();
 690:         let mut paths: Vec<&PathBuf> =
 691:             file_paths.iter().filter(|p| dict.contains(p)).collect();
 692:         paths.shuffle(&mut rng);
 693:         paths.into_iter().take(5).collect()
 694:     };
 695: 
 696:     let sample_size = sample.len() as u32;
 697:     info!("bootstrap: verifying {} templates", sample_size);
 698:     let mut failures = 0u32;
 699:     let mut failed_lines: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
 700: 
 701:     for rel_path in &sample {
 702:         let abs = source_root.join(rel_path);
 703:         let entry = match dict.get(rel_path) {
 704:             Some(e) => e,
 705:             None => continue,
 706:         };
 707: 
 708:         vscode::open_file(&abs)
 709:             .map_err(|e| io_err("9/9 verify templates", e))?;
 710:         sleep(Duration::from_millis(380)).await;
 711: 
 712:         let screenshot = screen::capture_screen()
 713:             .map_err(|e| io_err("9/9 verify templates", e))?;
 714:         let logical = scale_down(&screenshot, scale);
 715:         let sb = dict.sidebar_region;
 716:         let sidebar_img = logical.crop_imm(sb.x, sb.y, sb.width, sb.height);
 717: 
 718:         let result = template::find(&sidebar_img, &entry.template)
 719:             .map_err(|e| io_err("9/9 verify templates", e))?;
 720: 
 721:         if result.score >= dict.match_threshold {
 722:             info!("  ✓ {} score={:.2}", rel_path.display(), result.score);
 723:         } else {
 724:             warn!(
 725:                 "  ✗ {} score={:.2} (threshold={:.2})",
 726:                 rel_path.display(),
 727:                 result.score,
 728:                 dict.match_threshold
 729:             );
 730:             failed_lines.push(format!(
 731:                 "  • {}  (score={:.2})",
 732:                 rel_path.display(),
 733:                 result.score
 734:             ));
 735:             save_diagnostic_screenshot(
 736:                 diag_dir,
 737:                 &format!("verify_fail_{}", sanitize_filename(rel_path)),
 738:             );
 739:             failures += 1;
 740:         }
 741:     }
 742: 
 743:     if failures > MAX_VERIFY_FAILURES {
 744:         return Err(BootstrapError::VerificationFailed {
 745:             failures,
 746:             sample_size,
 747:             threshold: dict.match_threshold,
 748:             failed_list: failed_lines.join("\n"),
 749:             diag_dir: diag_dir.to_path_buf(),
 750:         });
 751:     }
 752: 
 753:     info!("bootstrap: verification passed");
 754:     Ok(())
 755: }
 756: 
 757: // ─── Template capture ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 758: 
 759: async fn capture_template(
 760:     abs_path: &Path,
 761:     dict: &VisualDictionary,
 762:     scale: f32,
 763: ) -> anyhow::Result<(DynamicImage, u32)> {
 764:     vscode::open_file(abs_path)?;
 765:     sleep(Duration::from_millis(380)).await;
 766: 
 767:     let screenshot = screen::capture_screen()?;
 768:     let logical = scale_down(&screenshot, scale);
 769:     let sb = dict.sidebar_region;
 770:     let sidebar_img = logical.crop_imm(sb.x, sb.y, sb.width, sb.height);
 771: 
 772:     let sel_y = find_selected_row(&sidebar_img)
 773:         .ok_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!("no selected row found in sidebar"))?;
 774:     let tmpl = crop_row(&sidebar_img, sel_y, dict.row_height_px);
 775:     let screen_y = sb.y + sel_y;
 776:     Ok((tmpl, screen_y))
 777: }
 778: 
 779: // ─── Parent directory template capture ───────────────────────────────────────
 780: 
 781: /// For each immediate parent directory not yet in the dictionary:
 782: ///   1. Open a direct child file (already on disk).
 783: ///   2. Focus the Explorer sidebar (Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+E).
 784: ///   3. Press Left Arrow — moves focus from file row to parent directory row.
 785: ///   4. Capture the highlighted row and store under the directory's relative path.
 786: ///
 787: /// Best-effort: failures are logged and skipped without aborting bootstrap.
 788: async fn capture_parent_dir_templates(
 789:     file_paths: &[PathBuf],
 790:     source_root: &Path,
 791:     dict: &mut VisualDictionary,
 792:     scale: f32,
 793: ) {
 794:     use std::collections::HashMap;
 795: 
 796:     // Walk every ancestor of every file path (not just immediate parents).
 797:     // For each ancestor directory, record the shallowest available child file
 798:     // so we can reach it with a single Left Arrow press from the file row.
 799:     // Key: ancestor dir  Value: (child file, depth = levels between child and dir)
 800:     let mut to_capture: HashMap<PathBuf, (PathBuf, usize)> = HashMap::new();
 801:     for fp in file_paths {
 802:         let mut current = fp.as_path();
 803:         while let Some(parent) = current.parent() {
 804:             if parent.as_os_str().is_empty() || parent == Path::new(".") {
 805:                 break;
 806:             }
 807:             let parent_path = parent.to_path_buf();
 808:             if !dict.contains(&parent_path) {
 809:                 let depth = fp.components().count()
 810:                     .saturating_sub(parent_path.components().count());
 811:                 to_capture
 812:                     .entry(parent_path)
 813:                     .and_modify(|(existing, ex_depth)| {
 814:                         if depth < *ex_depth {
 815:                             *existing = fp.to_path_buf();
 816:                             *ex_depth = depth;
 817:                         }
 818:                     })
 819:                     .or_insert_with(|| (fp.to_path_buf(), depth));
 820:             }
 821:             current = parent;
 822:         }
 823:     }
 824: 
 825:     if to_capture.is_empty() {
 826:         return;
 827:     }
 828:     info!("bootstrap: capturing {} parent directory templates", to_capture.len());
 829: 
 830:     // Sort shallowest-first so outer dirs are captured before inner dirs.
 831:     let mut sorted: Vec<_> = to_capture.into_iter().collect();
 832:     sorted.sort_by_key(|(dir, _)| dir.components().count());
 833: 
 834:     for (dir_path, (child_file, depth)) in &sorted {
 835:         if *depth > 1 {
 836:             // Left Arrow from a FOLDER row may collapse rather than navigate up.
 837:             // Only depth=1 (direct child) is reliable. Deeper ancestors without a
 838:             // direct child file are handled by ensure_parents_expanded's expand_all fallback.
 839:             warn!(
 840:                 "bootstrap: no direct child for {} (shallowest is {} levels deep) — \
 841:                  skipping, will use keyboard expand at runtime",
 842:                 dir_path.display(), depth
 843:             );
 844:             continue;
 845:         }
 846:         let abs_child = source_root.join(child_file);
 847:         if !abs_child.exists() {
 848:             continue;
 849:         }
 850: 
 851:         // Open child file — VSCode highlights it in Explorer.
 852:         if vscode::open_file(&abs_child).is_err() {
 853:             continue;
 854:         }
 855:         sleep(Duration::from_millis(350)).await;
 856: 
 857:         // Focus Explorer sidebar.
 858:         let focused = tokio::task::spawn_blocking(|| -> bool {
 859:             let Ok(mut enigo) = Enigo::new(&Settings::default()) else { return false };
 860:             #[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
 861:             let modifier = Key::Meta;
 862:             #[cfg(not(target_os = "macos"))]
 863:             let modifier = Key::Control;
 864:             enigo.key(modifier, enigo::Direction::Press).ok();
 865:             enigo.key(Key::Shift, enigo::Direction::Press).ok();
 866:             enigo.key(Key::Unicode('e'), enigo::Direction::Click).ok();
 867:             enigo.key(Key::Shift, enigo::Direction::Release).ok();
 868:             enigo.key(modifier, enigo::Direction::Release).ok();
 869:             true
 870:         })
 871:         .await
 872:         .unwrap_or(false);
 873: 
 874:         if !focused {
 875:             warn!("bootstrap: could not focus Explorer for dir {}", dir_path.display());
 876:             continue;
 877:         }
 878:         sleep(Duration::from_millis(200)).await;
 879: 
 880:         // Left Arrow: from file row → parent directory row.
 881:         let _ = tokio::task::spawn_blocking(|| -> bool {
 882:             let Ok(mut enigo) = Enigo::new(&Settings::default()) else { return false };
 883:             enigo.key(Key::LeftArrow, enigo::Direction::Click).ok();
 884:             true
 885:         })
 886:         .await
 887:         .unwrap_or(false);
 888:         sleep(Duration::from_millis(200)).await;
 889: 
 890:         // Capture the highlighted parent directory row.
 891:         let Ok(screenshot) = screen::capture_screen() else {
 892:             warn!("bootstrap: screenshot failed for dir {}", dir_path.display());
 893:             continue;
 894:         };
 895:         let logical = scale_down(&screenshot, scale);
 896:         let sb = dict.sidebar_region;
 897:         let sidebar_img = logical.crop_imm(sb.x, sb.y, sb.width, sb.height);
 898: 
 899:         if let Some(sel_y) = find_selected_row(&sidebar_img) {
 900:             let tmpl = crop_row(&sidebar_img, sel_y, dict.row_height_px);
 901:             let screen_y = sb.y + sel_y;
 902:             match dict.insert(dir_path, tmpl, Some(screen_y)) {
 903:                 Ok(()) => info!("bootstrap: captured dir template: {}", dir_path.display()),
 904:                 Err(e) => warn!("bootstrap: could not store dir template {}: {e}", dir_path.display()),
 905:             }
 906:         } else {
 907:             warn!("bootstrap: no highlighted row for dir {}", dir_path.display());
 908:         }
 909:     }
 910: }
 911: 
 912: // ─── Row height measurement ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
 913: 
 914: fn measure_row_height(y_samples: &[u32]) -> u32 {
 915:     if y_samples.len() < 2 {
 916:         return 22; // safe VSCode default
 917:     }
 918:     let mut sorted = y_samples.to_vec();
 919:     sorted.sort_unstable();
 920: 
 921:     let mut gaps: Vec<u32> = sorted
 922:         .windows(2)
 923:         .map(|w| w[1].saturating_sub(w[0]))
 924:         .filter(|&g| g >= 16 && g <= 40) // plausible row heights
 925:         .collect();
 926: 
 927:     if gaps.is_empty() {
 928:         return 22;
 929:     }
 930: 
 931:     gaps.sort_unstable();
 932:     gaps[gaps.len() / 2] // median
 933: }
 934: 
 935: // ─── Sidebar geometry helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
 936: 
 937: /// Scan horizontally from the activity bar right edge, looking for the
 938: /// colour transition that marks the sidebar–editor boundary.
 939: ///
 940: /// A transition is confirmed by `RUN_NEEDED` consecutive pixels that all
 941: /// differ from the sidebar background by more than `DIFF_THRESHOLD` luma.
 942: fn find_sidebar_right_edge(img: &DynamicImage) -> Option<u32> {
 943:     let max_x = (img.width() / 2).min(600);
 944:     let mid_y = img.height() / 2;
 945: 
 946:     let probe_x = ACTIVITY_BAR_WIDTH + 10;
 947:     if probe_x >= img.width() {
 948:         return None;
 949:     }
 950:     let base = pixel_luma(img, probe_x, mid_y);
 951: 
 952:     let mut run_start: Option<u32> = None;
 953:     let mut run_len: u32 = 0;
 954:     const RUN_NEEDED: u32 = 6;
 955:     const DIFF_THRESHOLD: i32 = 20;
 956: 
 957:     for x in (ACTIVITY_BAR_WIDTH + 40)..max_x {
 958:         let diff = (pixel_luma(img, x, mid_y) as i32 - base as i32).abs();
 959:         if diff > DIFF_THRESHOLD {
 960:             if run_start.is_none() {
 961:                 run_start = Some(x);
 962:                 run_len = 1;
 963:             } else {
 964:                 run_len += 1;
 965:                 if run_len >= RUN_NEEDED {
 966:                     return run_start;
 967:                 }
 968:             }
 969:         } else {
 970:             run_start = None;
 971:             run_len = 0;
 972:         }
 973:     }
 974:     None
 975: }
 976: 
 977: /// Check that a candidate sidebar region contains file-tree row structure.
 978: ///
 979: /// File rows produce horizontal variance (icon + text vs background).
 980: /// We require at least 3 high-variance rows in the top 300px.
 981: fn has_file_tree_structure(img: &DynamicImage, region: Rect) -> bool {
 982:     let mut text_rows = 0u32;
 983:     for y in (region.y..region.y + region.height.min(300)).step_by(4) {
 984:         if row_variance(img, region.x, region.x + region.width, y) > 200.0 {
 985:             text_rows += 1;
 986:         }
 987:     }
 988:     text_rows >= 3
 989: }
 990: 
 991: fn row_variance(img: &DynamicImage, x0: u32, x1: u32, y: u32) -> f64 {
 992:     if y >= img.height() {
 993:         return 0.0;
 994:     }
 995:     let samples: Vec<f64> = (x0..x1.min(img.width()))
 996:         .map(|x| pixel_luma(img, x, y) as f64)
 997:         .collect();
 998:     if samples.is_empty() {
 999:         return 0.0;
1000:     }
1001:     let mean = samples.iter().sum::<f64>() / samples.len() as f64;
1002:     samples.iter().map(|v| (v - mean).powi(2)).sum::<f64>() / samples.len() as f64
1003: }
1004: 
1005: fn pixel_luma(img: &DynamicImage, x: u32, y: u32) -> u8 {
1006:     let p = img.get_pixel(x.min(img.width() - 1), y.min(img.height() - 1));
1007:     ((p[0] as u16 * 77 + p[1] as u16 * 150 + p[2] as u16 * 29) >> 8) as u8
1008: }
1009: 
1010: // ─── Diagnostic helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
1011: 
1012: /// Save a full-screen screenshot to the diagnostics directory and return its path.
1013: ///
1014: /// Errors are swallowed — diagnostics are best-effort and must never prevent
1015: /// the primary error from propagating.
1016: fn save_diagnostic_screenshot(diag_dir: &Path, name: &str) -> PathBuf {
1017:     let path = diag_dir.join(format!("{name}.png"));
1018:     if let Ok(img) = screen::capture_screen() {
1019:         let _ = img.save(&path);
1020:     }
1021:     path
1022: }
1023: 
1024: /// Convert a relative file path to a safe filename fragment (≤ 40 chars).
1025: fn sanitize_filename(p: &Path) -> String {
1026:     p.to_string_lossy()
1027:         .replace(['/', '\\', '.', ' '], "_")
1028:         .chars()
1029:         .take(40)
1030:         .collect()
1031: }
1032: 
1033: // ─── Scale helper ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
1034: 
1035: fn scale_down(img: &DynamicImage, scale: f32) -> DynamicImage {
1036:     if (scale - 1.0).abs() < 0.01 {
1037:         return img.clone();
1038:     }
1039:     let w = (img.width() as f32 / scale) as u32;
1040:     let h = (img.height() as f32 / scale) as u32;
1041:     img.resize_exact(w, h, image::imageops::FilterType::Lanczos3)
1042: }
1043: 
1044: // ─── Error conversion helper ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
1045: 
1046: /// Convert any `anyhow::Error` or `std::io::Error` into a `BootstrapError::Io`.
1047: fn io_err(step: impl Into<String>, e: impl std::fmt::Display) -> BootstrapError {
1048:     BootstrapError::Io {
1049:         step: step.into(),
1050:         source: std::io::Error::new(std::io::ErrorKind::Other, e.to_string()),
1051:     }
1052: }

===== /src/navigator/bootstrap_error.rs =====

   1: //! Rich, actionable error types for the bootstrap sequence.
   2: //!
   3: //! Every variant explains:
   4: //!   1. What failed (concrete)
   5: //!   2. Why it most likely failed (top causes)
   6: //!   3. What the user can do right now
   7: //!   4. Where diagnostic screenshots are saved (when applicable)
   8: 
   9: use std::path::PathBuf;
  10: use thiserror::Error;
  11: 
  12: /// Step labels used in progress output and error messages.
  13: pub const STEPS: &[&str] = &[
  14:     "1/9  pre-flight checks",
  15:     "2/9  open VSCode",
  16:     "3/9  force fullscreen",
  17:     "4/9  detect DPI scale",
  18:     "5/9  open Explorer sidebar",
  19:     "6/9  detect sidebar bounds",
  20:     "7/9  expand file tree",
  21:     "8/9  capture file templates",
  22:     "9/9  verify templates",
  23: ];
  24: 
  25: #[derive(Debug, Error)]
  26: pub enum BootstrapError {
  27:     // ── Pre-flight ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  28: 
  29:     #[error(
  30:         "Bootstrap failed at step 1/9 — VSCode CLI (`code`) not found on PATH.\n\
  31:          \n\
  32:          Why this happened:\n\
  33:            • VSCode is not installed, or\n\
  34:            • the `code` command was not added to your shell PATH.\n\
  35:          \n\
  36:          How to fix:\n\
  37:            1. Open VSCode → Command Palette (Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+P)\n\
  38:            2. Run: \"Shell Command: Install 'code' command in PATH\"\n\
  39:            3. Restart your terminal, then try again.\n\
  40:          \n\
  41:          On macOS you can also add it manually:\n\
  42:            export PATH=\"$PATH:/Applications/Visual Studio Code.app/Contents/Resources/app/bin\""
  43:     )]
  44:     VscodeCliMissing,
  45: 
  46:     #[error(
  47:         "Bootstrap failed at step 1/9 — screen capture permission denied.\n\
  48:          \n\
  49:          The screenshot returned a fully black image, which means this application\n\
  50:          does not have permission to record the screen.\n\
  51:          \n\
  52:          How to fix (macOS):\n\
  53:            1. Open System Settings → Privacy & Security → Screen Recording\n\
  54:            2. Enable screen recording for your terminal (iTerm, Terminal.app, etc.)\n\
  55:            3. Restart the terminal completely, then run ghostwriter again.\n\
  56:          \n\
  57:          How to fix (Linux):\n\
  58:            • Ensure you are running in an X11 session (Wayland may block capture)\n\
  59:            • Try: DISPLAY=:0 ghostwriter ...\n\
  60:          \n\
  61:          How to fix (Windows):\n\
  62:            • Run the terminal as Administrator if in a restricted environment."
  63:     )]
  64:     ScreenCapturePermissionDenied,
  65: 
  66:     #[error(
  67:         "Bootstrap failed at step 1/9 — accessibility / input injection permission denied.\n\
  68:          \n\
  69:          ghostwriter needs to move the mouse and type keystrokes, but the OS\n\
  70:          blocked the input injection attempt.\n\
  71:          \n\
  72:          How to fix (macOS):\n\
  73:            1. Open System Settings → Privacy & Security → Accessibility\n\
  74:            2. Enable your terminal application (iTerm, Terminal.app, etc.)\n\
  75:            3. Restart the terminal, then run ghostwriter again.\n\
  76:          \n\
  77:          How to fix (Linux):\n\
  78:            • Ensure X11 is running and DISPLAY is set correctly.\n\
  79:          \n\
  80:          How to fix (Windows):\n\
  81:            • Run the terminal as Administrator."
  82:     )]
  83:     InputInjectionPermissionDenied,
  84: 
  85:     #[error(
  86:         "Bootstrap failed at step 1/9 — `wmctrl` is not installed.\n\
  87:          \n\
  88:          ghostwriter uses wmctrl to bring the VSCode window to the foreground on Linux.\n\
  89:          \n\
  90:          How to fix:\n\
  91:            Ubuntu/Debian:  sudo apt install wmctrl\n\
  92:            Fedora:         sudo dnf install wmctrl\n\
  93:            Arch:           sudo pacman -S wmctrl"
  94:     )]
  95:     WmctrlMissing,
  96: 
  97:     // ── Step 2: Open VSCode ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
  98: 
  99:     #[error(
 100:         "Bootstrap failed at step 2/9 — VSCode did not become visible after {waited_ms}ms.\n\
 101:          \n\
 102:          ghostwriter ran `code {path}` but could not detect a VSCode window\n\
 103:          on screen.\n\
 104:          \n\
 105:          Why this happened:\n\
 106:            • VSCode may be loading slowly (first launch, large extension set)\n\
 107:            • VSCode may have opened on a different monitor\n\
 108:            • The window may be minimised or behind another app\n\
 109:          \n\
 110:          How to fix:\n\
 111:            • Bring VSCode to the foreground manually and run ghostwriter again.\n\
 112:            • Or increase the launch timeout with --launch-timeout-ms 8000\n\
 113:          \n\
 114:          Diagnostic screenshot saved to: {diag_path}"
 115:     )]
 116:     VscodeNotVisible {
 117:         waited_ms: u64,
 118:         path: PathBuf,
 119:         diag_path: PathBuf,
 120:     },
 121: 
 122:     // ── Step 3: Fullscreen ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
 123: 
 124:     #[error(
 125:         "Bootstrap failed at step 3/9 — could not force VSCode into fullscreen.\n\
 126:          \n\
 127:          After sending the fullscreen shortcut twice, the window area is still\n\
 128:          smaller than the display ({window_area} < {display_area} pixels).\n\
 129:          \n\
 130:          Why this matters:\n\
 131:            ghostwriter measures pixel coordinates during bootstrap. If VSCode is not\n\
 132:            fullscreen, the sidebar width will differ between bootstrap and playback,\n\
 133:            causing all template matches to fail.\n\
 134:          \n\
 135:          How to fix:\n\
 136:            1. Manually maximise/fullscreen VSCode before running ghostwriter.\n\
 137:            2. On macOS: green button at top-left of window → \"Enter Full Screen\"\n\
 138:            3. On Windows/Linux: press F11 inside VSCode.\n\
 139:            4. Then run ghostwriter again.\n\
 140:          \n\
 141:          Diagnostic screenshot saved to: {diag_path}"
 142:     )]
 143:     FullscreenFailed {
 144:         window_area: u32,
 145:         display_area: u32,
 146:         diag_path: PathBuf,
 147:     },
 148: 
 149:     // ── Step 5: Explorer sidebar ─────────────────────────────────────────────
 150: 
 151:     #[error(
 152:         "Bootstrap failed at step 5/9 — Explorer sidebar did not open after {attempts} attempts.\n\
 153:          \n\
 154:          ghostwriter sent Ctrl+Shift+E and also tried clicking the activity bar\n\
 155:          icon, but the sidebar region shows no file tree.\n\
 156:          \n\
 157:          Why this happened:\n\
 158:            • The Explorer view may be permanently hidden via View → Appearance\n\
 159:            • A VSCode extension may be intercepting Ctrl+Shift+E\n\
 160:            • The keyboard shortcut may have been remapped in your keybindings\n\
 161:            • The window may have lost focus between the shortcut and the check\n\
 162:          \n\
 163:          How to fix:\n\
 164:            1. Open VSCode manually and ensure the Explorer sidebar is visible.\n\
 165:            2. Make sure no other app intercepts Ctrl+Shift+E.\n\
 166:            3. If the shortcut is remapped: open the Explorer manually first,\n\
 167:               then run ghostwriter again.\n\
 168:          \n\
 169:          Diagnostic screenshot saved to: {diag_path}"
 170:     )]
 171:     ExplorerNotOpen {
 172:         attempts: u8,
 173:         diag_path: PathBuf,
 174:     },
 175: 
 176:     // ── Step 6: Sidebar bounds ───────────────────────────────────────────────
 177: 
 178:     #[error(
 179:         "Bootstrap failed at step 6/9 — could not determine sidebar boundaries.\n\
 180:          \n\
 181:          The sidebar detection scan (left {scan_width}px of screen) did not find\n\
 182:          a clear colour boundary between the sidebar and the editor.\n\
 183:          \n\
 184:          Why this happened:\n\
 185:            • Your VSCode theme uses identical background colours for the sidebar\n\
 186:              and editor (e.g. some minimal/transparent themes)\n\
 187:            • The sidebar may be very narrow (<80px)\n\
 188:            • A panel overlay (search results, notifications) may be covering the\n\
 189:              sidebar-editor boundary\n\
 190:          \n\
 191:          How to fix:\n\
 192:            1. Switch to a standard theme: Dark+ or Light+ (File → Preferences → Theme)\n\
 193:            2. Ensure the sidebar is a reasonable width (drag to widen if needed)\n\
 194:            3. Dismiss any panels or overlays in the VSCode window\n\
 195:            4. Run ghostwriter again.\n\
 196:          \n\
 197:          Diagnostic screenshot saved to: {diag_path}"
 198:     )]
 199:     SidebarBoundsNotFound {
 200:         scan_width: u32,
 201:         diag_path: PathBuf,
 202:     },
 203: 
 204:     #[error(
 205:         "Bootstrap failed at step 6/9 — sidebar region contains no file tree structure.\n\
 206:          \n\
 207:          A sidebar region was detected (x={x}, w={width}px) but it appears\n\
 208:          empty — no file rows were found inside it.\n\
 209:          \n\
 210:          Why this happened:\n\
 211:            • The repository folder may not have loaded yet in the Explorer\n\
 212:            • The Explorer may be showing an empty \"Open Folder\" prompt instead\n\
 213:              of a file tree\n\
 214:            • VSCode may have opened with a different workspace/folder\n\
 215:          \n\
 216:          How to fix:\n\
 217:            1. Ensure VSCode has opened the correct folder and the file tree\n\
 218:               is populated before running ghostwriter.\n\
 219:            2. Wait a few seconds for VSCode to finish indexing, then try again.\n\
 220:          \n\
 221:          Diagnostic screenshot saved to: {diag_path}"
 222:     )]
 223:     SidebarEmpty {
 224:         x: u32,
 225:         width: u32,
 226:         diag_path: PathBuf,
 227:     },
 228: 
 229:     // ── Step 7: Expand tree ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
 230: 
 231:     #[error(
 232:         "Bootstrap failed at step 7/9 — could not expand the file tree.\n\
 233:          \n\
 234:          The Command Palette command \"Explorer: Expand All\" did not complete\n\
 235:          successfully.\n\
 236:          \n\
 237:          Why this happened:\n\
 238:            • The Command Palette may have been intercepted or not opened\n\
 239:            • The command name may differ in your VSCode version (rare)\n\
 240:            • The keyboard shortcut Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+P may be remapped\n\
 241:          \n\

 243:            1. Open VSCode, manually expand all folders in the Explorer, then\n\
 244:               run ghostwriter again.\n\
 245:            2. Alternatively: disable any extension that might intercept\n\
 246:               Ctrl+Shift+P.\n\
 247:          \n\
 248:          Diagnostic screenshot saved to: {diag_path}"
 249:     )]
 250:     ExpandAllFailed { diag_path: PathBuf },
 251: 
 252:     // ── Step 8: Template capture ─────────────────────────────────────────────
 253: 
 254:     #[error(
 255:         "Bootstrap failed at step 8/9 — too few templates captured.\n\
 256:          \n\
 257:          Captured {captured}/{total} templates, but the minimum required is\n\
 258:          {min_required} ({min_pct}% of files).\n\
 259:          \n\
 260:          Files that failed to capture:\n\
 261:          {failed_list}\n\
 262:          \n\
 263:          Why this happened:\n\
 264:            • Some files may not be visible in the tree (inside collapsed folders\n\
 265:              that did not expand in step 7)\n\
 266:            • The selected-row highlight may not be detectable with your theme\n\
 267:            • VSCode may be lagging and the highlight appears before ghostwriter\n\
 268:              takes the screenshot\n\
 269:          \n\
 270:          How to fix:\n\
 271:            1. Try switching to the Dark+ or Light+ theme.\n\
 272:            2. Increase the capture delay with --capture-delay-ms 600\n\
 273:            3. Manually expand all folders and run again.\n\
 274:          \n\
 275:          Diagnostic screenshots saved to: {diag_dir}"
 276:     )]
 277:     TooFewTemplates {
 278:         captured: usize,
 279:         total: usize,
 280:         min_required: usize,
 281:         min_pct: u8,
 282:         failed_list: String,
 283:         diag_dir: PathBuf,
 284:     },
 285: 
 286:     // ── Step 9: Verification ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
 287: 
 288:     #[error(
 289:         "Bootstrap failed at step 9/9 — template verification failed.\n\
 290:          \n\
 291:          {failures}/{sample_size} re-opened files scored below the NCC\n\
 292:          threshold of {threshold:.2}. The visual dictionary is not reliable\n\
 293:          enough to drive navigation.\n\
 294:          \n\
 295:          Failed files (with scores):\n\
 296:          {failed_list}\n\
 297:          \n\
 298:          Why this happened:\n\
 299:            • The sidebar shifted slightly between capture and verification\n\
 300:              (e.g. scrollbar appeared, or a notification changed layout)\n\
 301:            • Your VSCode theme renders file icons that vary at low confidence\n\
 302:            • The DPI scale factor may be fractional (e.g. 1.25×) causing\n\
 303:              sub-pixel alignment issues\n\
 304:          \n\
 305:          How to fix:\n\
 306:            1. Delete .ghostwriter/ and run ghostwriter again to recalibrate.\n\
 307:            2. Lower the threshold with --match-threshold 0.75 (less strict).\n\
 308:            3. Switch to Dark+ or Light+ theme for more consistent rendering.\n\
 309:          \n\
 310:          Diagnostic screenshots saved to: {diag_dir}"
 311:     )]
 312:     VerificationFailed {
 313:         failures: u32,
 314:         sample_size: u32,
 315:         threshold: f32,
 316:         failed_list: String,
 317:         diag_dir: PathBuf,
 318:     },
 319: 
 320:     // ── Misc ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 321: 
 322:     #[error(
 323:         "Bootstrap failed — could not save the visual dictionary.\n\
 324:          \n\
 325:          Path: {path}\n\
 326:          Reason: {reason}\n\
 327:          \n\
 328:          How to fix:\n\
 329:            • Ensure the destination directory is writable.\n\
 330:            • Check available disk space.\n\
 331:            • If .ghostwriter/ exists with wrong permissions: rm -rf .ghostwriter/"
 332:     )]
 333:     DictionarySaveFailed { path: PathBuf, reason: String },
 334:
