 337:          \n\
 338:          Details: {source}\n\
 339:          \n\
 340:          If this error persists, please report it with the .ghostwriter/diagnostics/\n\
 341:          folder attached."
 342:     )]
 343:     Io {
 344:         step: String,
 345:         #[source]
 346:         source: std::io::Error,
 347:     },
 348: 
 349:     #[error(transparent)]
 350:     Other(#[from] anyhow::Error),
 351: }
 352: 
 353: impl BootstrapError {
 354:     /// Print a formatted error box to stderr so the user can't miss it.
 355:     pub fn print_fatal(&self) {
 356:         let border = "═".repeat(70);
 357:         eprintln!("\n╔{border}╗");
 358:         eprintln!("║  GHOSTWRITER BOOTSTRAP ERROR{:41}║", "");
 359:         eprintln!("╠{border}╣");
 360:         for line in self.to_string().lines() {
 361:             eprintln!("║  {line:<68}║");
 362:         }
 363:         eprintln!("╚{border}╝\n");
 364:     }
 365: }

===== /src/navigator/calibrator.rs =====

   1: //! Post-playback template capture for newly created files.
   2: //!
   3: //! During playback the engine creates files that did not exist at bootstrap time.
   4: //! `capture_new_file` detects the highlighted row in the Explorer sidebar and
   5: //! stores a template so the Navigator can find the file on subsequent opens.
   6: 
   7: use crate::navigator::{
   8:     dictionary::VisualDictionary,
   9:     screen::capture_screen,
  10:     sidebar::{crop_row, find_selected_row},
  11: };
  12: use anyhow::Result;
  13: use image::DynamicImage;
  14: use tokio::time::{sleep, Duration};
  15: use tracing::{info, warn};
  16: 
  17: /// Capture the template for a single file that was just created during playback.
  18: ///
  19: /// Called by the engine immediately after creating a new file so that the
  20: /// file is registered in the dictionary for future navigation.
  21: pub async fn capture_new_file(
  22:     rel_path: &std::path::Path,
  23:     dict: &mut VisualDictionary,
  24:     scale: f32,
  25: ) -> Result<()> {
  26:     // VSCode usually selects a new file in the tree automatically.
  27:     sleep(Duration::from_millis(350)).await;
  28: 
  29:     let screenshot = capture_screen()?;
  30:     let logical = scale_down(&screenshot, scale);
  31:     let sidebar_img = logical.crop_imm(
  32:         dict.sidebar_region.x,
  33:         dict.sidebar_region.y,
  34:         dict.sidebar_region.width,
  35:         dict.sidebar_region.height,
  36:     );
  37: 
  38:     if let Some(sel_y) = find_selected_row(&sidebar_img) {
  39:         let template = crop_row(&sidebar_img, sel_y, dict.row_height_px);
  40:         let screen_y = dict.sidebar_region.y + sel_y;
  41:         dict.insert(rel_path, template, Some(screen_y))?;
  42:         dict.save()?;
  43:         info!("auto-captured new file: {} at y={}", rel_path.display(), screen_y);
  44:     } else {
  45:         warn!("could not auto-capture new file: {}", rel_path.display());
  46:     }
  47: 
  48:     Ok(())
  49: }
  50: 
  51: fn scale_down(img: &DynamicImage, scale: f32) -> DynamicImage {
  52:     if (scale - 1.0).abs() < 0.01 {
  53:         return img.clone();
  54:     }
  55:     let w = (img.width() as f32 / scale) as u32;
  56:     let h = (img.height() as f32 / scale) as u32;
  57:     img.resize_exact(w, h, image::imageops::FilterType::Lanczos3)
  58: }

===== /src/navigator/confirm.rs =====

   1: //! Post-action confirmation predicates.
   2: //!
   3: //! After a visual operation acts (clicks, types, etc.), the engine takes a
   4: //! fresh screenshot and runs one of these predicates against it. The
   5: //! predicate returns `Ok(())` if the post-state matches expectations, or
   6: //! `Err(ConfirmError)` describing what's wrong. The recovery layer in
   7: //! `visual_op` decides how to react to each failure type.
   8: //!
   9: //! Predicates are factory functions that capture their parameters and
  10: //! return a `ConfirmPredicate` (`Box<dyn FnMut(&DynamicImage) -> ...>`).
  11: //! `FnMut` is used so the same op can be retried after a recovery rung
  12: //! without rebuilding the predicate.
  13: //!
  14: //! See `docs/VISUAL_VERIFICATION.md` §6 for the full predicate catalogue.
  15: 
  16: use crate::navigator::screen::Rect;
  17: use crate::navigator::{bootstrap, sidebar, template};
  18: use image::DynamicImage;
  19: 
  20: // ─── Failure type ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  21: 
  22: /// Reasons a post-action confirmation can fail.
  23: ///
  24: /// Each variant maps to a different family of recovery strategies — see the
  25: /// recovery ladder in `visual_op.rs`.
  26: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]
  27: pub enum ConfirmError {
  28:     /// The act produced no observable change. Most common cause: focus
  29:     /// drifted away from VSCode, or the click landed outside the target.
  30:     PostStateUnchanged { reason: String },
  31: 
  32:     /// Something happened, but not what we wanted (wrong file open, wrong
  33:     /// folder expanded). Recovery typically rolls back via Esc.
  34:     WrongPostState { saw: String },
  35: 
  36:     /// Half-completed state (e.g. command palette dismissed but a modal is
  37:     /// now visible instead). Recovery is op-specific.
  38:     PartialPostState { detail: String },
  39: }
  40: 
  41: impl std::fmt::Display for ConfirmError {
  42:     fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
  43:         match self {
  44:             Self::PostStateUnchanged { reason } => write!(f, "post-state unchanged: {reason}"),
  45:             Self::WrongPostState { saw } => write!(f, "wrong post-state: {saw}"),
  46:             Self::PartialPostState { detail } => write!(f, "partial post-state: {detail}"),
  47:         }
  48:     }
  49: }
  50: 
  51: /// Type alias for a stateful predicate. `FnMut` so the op runner can call it
  52: /// across multiple attempts (after recovery rungs) without rebuilding.
  53: pub type ConfirmPredicate = Box<dyn FnMut(&DynamicImage) -> Result<(), ConfirmError>>;
  54: 
  55: // ─── Built-in predicates ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  56: 
  57: /// Confirm that a file's row template is visible in the sidebar at or above
  58: /// `threshold` score.
  59: ///
  60: /// Used after `Navigator::open(rel_path)` clicks. If the click landed on the
  61: /// right row, VSCode highlights it and our (highlighted-state) template
  62: /// matches well; if focus drifted or we missed by a row, score drops.
  63: pub fn selected_row_at(
  64:     template_img: DynamicImage,
  65:     sb_region: Rect,
  66:     threshold: f32,
  67: ) -> ConfirmPredicate {
  68:     Box::new(move |snapshot: &DynamicImage| {
  69:         if !crop_in_bounds(snapshot, sb_region) {
  70:             return Err(ConfirmError::WrongPostState {
  71:                 saw: format!(
  72:                     "snapshot {}×{} too small to crop sidebar at {:?}",
  73:                     snapshot.width(), snapshot.height(), sb_region
  74:                 ),
  75:             });
  76:         }
  77:         let sb_img = snapshot.crop_imm(sb_region.x, sb_region.y, sb_region.width, sb_region.height);
  78:         match template::find(&sb_img, &template_img) {
  79:             Ok(r) if r.score >= threshold => Ok(()),
  80:             Ok(r) => Err(ConfirmError::PostStateUnchanged {
  81:                 reason: format!(
  82:                     "row template scored {:.2} (need ≥ {:.2})",
  83:                     r.score, threshold
  84:                 ),
  85:             }),
  86:             Err(e) => Err(ConfirmError::WrongPostState {
  87:                 saw: format!("template::find failed: {e}"),
  88:             }),
  89:         }
  90:     })
  91: }
  92: 
  93: /// Confirm that a folder is now expanded (arrow pointing down) at the spot
  94: /// where its template matched.
  95: ///
  96: /// Used after `ensure_parents_expanded` clicks a collapsed folder. The
  97: /// predicate re-finds the folder template in the current snapshot, then
  98: /// asks `sidebar::is_collapsed` — if it returns `false`, we're expanded.
  99: pub fn folder_expanded(
 100:     template_img: DynamicImage,
 101:     sb_region: Rect,
 102:     threshold: f32,
 103: ) -> ConfirmPredicate {
 104:     let template_w = template_img.width();
 105:     let template_h = template_img.height();
 106:     Box::new(move |snapshot: &DynamicImage| {
 107:         if !crop_in_bounds(snapshot, sb_region) {
 108:             return Err(ConfirmError::WrongPostState {
 109:                 saw: format!(
 110:                     "snapshot {}×{} too small to crop sidebar at {:?}",
 111:                     snapshot.width(), snapshot.height(), sb_region
 112:                 ),
 113:             });
 114:         }
 115:         let sb_img = snapshot.crop_imm(sb_region.x, sb_region.y, sb_region.width, sb_region.height);
 116:         match template::find(&sb_img, &template_img) {
 117:             Ok(r) if r.score >= threshold => {
 118:                 let (cx, cy) = r.centre(template_w, template_h);
 119:                 if sidebar::is_collapsed(&sb_img, cx, cy) {
 120:                     Err(ConfirmError::PostStateUnchanged {
 121:                         reason: format!(
 122:                             "folder still collapsed at ({cx},{cy})"
 123:                         ),
 124:                     })
 125:                 } else {
 126:                     Ok(())
 127:                 }
 128:             }
 129:             Ok(r) => Err(ConfirmError::PartialPostState {
 130:                 detail: format!(
 131:                     "folder template scored {:.2} (need ≥ {:.2}); arrow state unknown",
 132:                     r.score, threshold
 133:                 ),
 134:             }),
 135:             Err(e) => Err(ConfirmError::WrongPostState {
 136:                 saw: format!("template::find failed: {e}"),
 137:             }),
 138:         }
 139:     })
 140: }
 141: 
 142: /// Confirm that the Command Palette is no longer visible.
 143: ///
 144: /// Used after `expand_all_folders` types and submits a command — the palette
 145: /// must have dismissed for the command to have actually executed.
 146: pub fn command_palette_closed() -> ConfirmPredicate {
 147:     Box::new(|snapshot: &DynamicImage| {
 148:         if bootstrap::looks_like_command_palette_open(snapshot) {
 149:             Err(ConfirmError::PartialPostState {
 150:                 detail: "command palette still appears open".into(),
 151:             })
 152:         } else {
 153:             Ok(())
 154:         }
 155:     })
 156: }
 157: 
 158: /// Confirm that the sidebar has SOME row highlighted (focus indicator visible).
 159: ///
 160: /// Used after a sidebar-focus shortcut (e.g. Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+E) where we don't
 161: /// care which specific row, just that the sidebar received focus.
 162: ///
 163: /// Not yet wired to a call site — built ahead of the bootstrap step-5 migration.
 164: #[allow(dead_code)]
 165: pub fn sidebar_focused(sb_region: Rect) -> ConfirmPredicate {
 166:     Box::new(move |snapshot: &DynamicImage| {
 167:         if !crop_in_bounds(snapshot, sb_region) {
 168:             return Err(ConfirmError::WrongPostState {
 169:                 saw: format!(
 170:                     "snapshot {}×{} too small to crop sidebar at {:?}",
 171:                     snapshot.width(), snapshot.height(), sb_region
 172:                 ),
 173:             });
 174:         }
 175:         let sb_img = snapshot.crop_imm(sb_region.x, sb_region.y, sb_region.width, sb_region.height);
 176:         if sidebar::find_selected_row(&sb_img).is_some() {
 177:             Ok(())
 178:         } else {
 179:             Err(ConfirmError::PostStateUnchanged {
 180:                 reason: "no highlighted row in sidebar".into(),
 181:             })
 182:         }
 183:     })
 184: }
 185: 
 186: // ─── Helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 187: 
 188: fn crop_in_bounds(snapshot: &DynamicImage, region: Rect) -> bool {
 189:     region.x + region.width <= snapshot.width()
 190:         && region.y + region.height <= snapshot.height()
 191: }
 192: 
 193: // ─── Tests ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 194: //
 195: // All predicates are exercised against synthesised images. The synth helpers
 196: // build the smallest image needed to drive a specific predicate path.
 197: 
 198: #[cfg(test)]
 199: mod tests {
 200:     use super::*;
 201:     use image::{Rgba, RgbaImage};
 202: 
 203:     fn solid(w: u32, h: u32, luma: u8) -> DynamicImage {
 204:         let mut img = RgbaImage::new(w, h);
 205:         for p in img.pixels_mut() {
 206:             *p = Rgba([luma, luma, luma, 255]);
 207:         }
 208:         DynamicImage::ImageRgba8(img)
 209:     }
 210: 
 211:     /// Build a 200×200 image with a distinctive 30×10 row pattern at (10, 50).
 212:     /// Returns the image and the cropped row to use as a template.
 213:     fn row_synth() -> (DynamicImage, DynamicImage) {
 214:         let mut img = RgbaImage::new(200, 200);
 215:         for p in img.pixels_mut() {
 216:             *p = Rgba([60, 60, 60, 255]);
 217:         }
 218:         // Distinctive pattern in 30×10 patch at (10, 50).
 219:         for y in 50u32..60 {
 220:             for x in 10u32..40 {
 221:                 let v = ((x.wrapping_mul(13)) % 200 + 30) as u8;
 222:                 img.put_pixel(x, y, Rgba([v, v, v, 255]));
 223:             }
 224:         }
 225:         let img = DynamicImage::ImageRgba8(img);
 226:         let template = img.crop_imm(10, 50, 30, 10);
 227:         (img, template)
 228:     }
 229: 
 230:     // ── selected_row_at ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 231: 
 232:     #[test]
 233:     fn selected_row_at_passes_when_template_is_visible() {
 234:         let (snap, template) = row_synth();
 235:         let sb = Rect::new(0, 0, 200, 200);
 236:         let mut pred = selected_row_at(template, sb, 0.82);
 237:         assert!(pred(&snap).is_ok(), "exact-match snapshot must pass");
 238:     }
 239: 
 240:     #[test]
 241:     fn selected_row_at_fails_post_state_unchanged_when_template_missing() {
 242:         // NCC of a distinctive template against a uniform haystack returns
 243:         // surprisingly high scores (~0.91 in practice) because the L2
 244:         // normalisation has a non-zero denominator from the haystack patch
 245:         // luma. We use a tight threshold (0.99) here so the predicate sees
 246:         // the score as below threshold — production VSCode haystacks have
 247:         // enough texture that 0.82 discriminates real matches.
 248:         let (_snap_with, template) = row_synth();
 249:         let blank = solid(200, 200, 60);
 250:         let sb = Rect::new(0, 0, 200, 200);
 251:         let mut pred = selected_row_at(template, sb, 0.99);
 252:         match pred(&blank) {
 253:             Err(ConfirmError::PostStateUnchanged { reason }) => {
 254:                 assert!(reason.contains("scored"), "unexpected reason: {reason}");
 255:             }
 256:             other => panic!("expected PostStateUnchanged, got {other:?}"),
 257:         }
 258:     }
 259: 
 260:     #[test]
 261:     fn selected_row_at_reports_wrong_state_when_sidebar_outside_snapshot() {
 262:         let (_, template) = row_synth();
 263:         let snap = solid(50, 50, 60); // smaller than sidebar region
 264:         let sb = Rect::new(0, 0, 200, 200);
 265:         let mut pred = selected_row_at(template, sb, 0.82);
 266:         assert!(matches!(pred(&snap), Err(ConfirmError::WrongPostState { .. })));
 267:     }
 268: 
 269:     // ── folder_expanded ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 270: 
 271:     /// Build a 200×100 sidebar where:
 272:     ///   - A "folder row" pattern lives at y=20..30 across the full width.
 273:     ///   - The arrow influence regions (right and below) are filled per the
 274:     ///     `state` parameter so `is_collapsed` reads them correctly.
 275:     fn folder_synth(state: &str) -> DynamicImage {
 276:         let mut img = RgbaImage::new(200, 100);
 277:         for p in img.pixels_mut() {
 278:             *p = Rgba([60, 60, 60, 255]);
 279:         }
 280:         // Distinctive row pattern across the row.
 281:         for y in 20u32..30 {
 282:             for x in 0u32..200 {
 283:                 let v = ((x.wrapping_mul(7)) % 180 + 30) as u8;
 284:                 img.put_pixel(x, y, Rgba([v, v, v, 255]));
 285:             }
 286:         }
 287:         // Arrow regions. With matched centre at (100, 25):
 288:         //   ax = 92, ay = 25
 289:         //   right region: (96..102, 25..29)  -- inside row
 290:         //   down  region: (92..96, 29..35)   -- below row (mostly)
 291:         let (right, down) = match state {
 292:             "expanded"  => (40u8, 220u8),  // down brighter than right
 293:             "collapsed" => (220u8, 40u8),  // right brighter than down
 294:             "neutral"   => (130u8, 130u8), // template-capture state
 295:             _ => panic!("unknown state {state}"),
 296:         };
 297:         for y in 25..29 {
 298:             for x in 96..102 {
 299:                 img.put_pixel(x, y, Rgba([right, right, right, 255]));
 300:             }
 301:         }
 302:         for y in 29..35 {
 303:             for x in 92..96 {
 304:                 img.put_pixel(x, y, Rgba([down, down, down, 255]));
 305:             }
 306:         }
 307:         DynamicImage::ImageRgba8(img)
 308:     }
 309: 
 310:     #[test]
 311:     fn folder_expanded_passes_when_arrow_points_down() {
 312:         let snap = folder_synth("expanded");
 313:         // Use the same expanded snapshot's row as the template — an exact match.
 314:         let template = snap.crop_imm(0, 20, 200, 10);
 315:         let sb = Rect::new(0, 0, 200, 100);
 316:         let mut pred = folder_expanded(template, sb, 0.5);
 317:         assert!(pred(&snap).is_ok(), "expanded folder must pass");
 318:     }
 319: 
 320:     #[test]
 321:     fn folder_expanded_fails_when_arrow_points_right() {
 322:         let snap = folder_synth("collapsed");
 323:         let template = snap.crop_imm(0, 20, 200, 10);
 324:         let sb = Rect::new(0, 0, 200, 100);
 325:         let mut pred = folder_expanded(template, sb, 0.5);
 326:         match pred(&snap) {
 327:             Err(ConfirmError::PostStateUnchanged { reason }) => {
 328:                 assert!(reason.contains("collapsed"), "unexpected reason: {reason}");
 329:             }
 330:             other => panic!("expected PostStateUnchanged, got {other:?}"),
 331:         }
 332:     }
 333: 
 334:     #[test]
 335:     fn folder_expanded_returns_partial_when_template_score_low() {
 336:         // Tight threshold (0.99) so the uniform-haystack NCC artefact
 337:         // (~0.91) lands below it — see selected_row_at_fails_… for why.
 338:         let snap = solid(200, 100, 60);
 339:         let (_, template) = {
 340:             let s = folder_synth("collapsed");
 341:             let t = s.crop_imm(0, 20, 200, 10);
 342:             (s, t)
 343:         };
 344:         let sb = Rect::new(0, 0, 200, 100);
 345:         let mut pred = folder_expanded(template, sb, 0.99);
 346:         assert!(matches!(pred(&snap), Err(ConfirmError::PartialPostState { .. })),
 347:                 "uniform snapshot should yield PartialPostState (template invisible)");
 348:     }
 349: 
 350:     // ── command_palette_closed ───────────────────────────────────────────────
 351: 
 352:     #[test]
 353:     fn command_palette_closed_passes_on_uniform_image() {
 354:         let snap = solid(800, 600, 40);
 355:         let mut pred = command_palette_closed();
 356:         assert!(pred(&snap).is_ok());
 357:     }
 358: 
 359:     #[test]
 360:     fn command_palette_closed_fails_when_palette_visible() {
 361:         // Synth a screenshot with a bright box near the top centre — the
 362:         // signature `looks_like_command_palette_open` looks for.
 363:         let mut buf = RgbaImage::new(800, 600);
 364:         for p in buf.pixels_mut() {
 365:             *p = Rgba([40, 40, 40, 255]);
 366:         }
 367:         // Bright palette box around y=60, centre-x.
 368:         for y in 40..100 {
 369:             for x in 300..500 {
 370:                 buf.put_pixel(x, y, Rgba([220, 220, 220, 255]));
 371:             }
 372:         }
 373:         let snap = DynamicImage::ImageRgba8(buf);
 374:         let mut pred = command_palette_closed();
 375:         assert!(matches!(pred(&snap), Err(ConfirmError::PartialPostState { .. })));
 376:     }
 377: 
 378:     // ── sidebar_focused ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 379: 
 380:     fn sidebar_with_highlight(highlight_y: Option<u32>) -> DynamicImage {
 381:         let mut img = RgbaImage::new(200, 400);
 382:         for p in img.pixels_mut() {
 383:             *p = Rgba([40, 40, 40, 255]);
 384:         }
 385:         if let Some(y) = highlight_y {
 386:             for yy in y..(y + 22).min(400) {
 387:                 for x in 0..200 {
 388:                     img.put_pixel(x, yy, Rgba([90, 100, 140, 255]));
 389:                 }
 390:             }
 391:         }
 392:         DynamicImage::ImageRgba8(img)
 393:     }
 394:

 400:         assert!(pred(&snap).is_ok());
 401:     }

 406:         let sb = Rect::new(0, 0, 200, 400);
 407:         let mut pred = sidebar_focused(sb);
 408:         assert!(matches!(pred(&snap), Err(ConfirmError::PostStateUnchanged { .. })));
 409:     }
 410: 
 411:     // ── Predicates are reusable across calls (FnMut) ─────────────────────────
 412: 
 413:     #[test]
 414:     fn predicates_can_be_called_multiple_times() {
 415:         let snap = sidebar_with_highlight(Some(40));
 416:         let sb = Rect::new(0, 0, 200, 400);
 417:         let mut pred = sidebar_focused(sb);
 418:         // Three back-to-back calls all return the same outcome.
 419:         assert!(pred(&snap).is_ok());
 420:         assert!(pred(&snap).is_ok());
 421:         assert!(pred(&snap).is_ok());
 422:     }

 425: 
 426:     #[test]
 427:     fn confirm_error_display_includes_the_reason() {
 428:         let e = ConfirmError::PostStateUnchanged { reason: "abc".into() };
 429:         assert!(format!("{e}").contains("abc"));
 430:         let e = ConfirmError::WrongPostState { saw: "xyz".into() };
 431:         assert!(format!("{e}").contains("xyz"));
 432:         let e = ConfirmError::PartialPostState { detail: "qrs".into() };
 433:         assert!(format!("{e}").contains("qrs"));
 434:     }
