===== /tests/helpers/fixture_repo.rs =====

   1: use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
   2: 
   3: /// Copy the tiny_repo fixture to a fresh tempdir so tests cannot mutate it.
   4: /// Returns `(TempDir, path_to_repo_root)` — the `TempDir` must be kept alive
   5: /// for the duration of the test.
   6: pub fn copy_tiny_repo() -> (tempfile::TempDir, PathBuf) {
   7:     let dir = tempfile::tempdir().expect("create tempdir");
   8:     // Place the copy in a named subdirectory so orchestrate() derives the
   9:     // correct project name ("tiny_repo") from the directory's file_name().
  10:     let repo = dir.path().join("tiny_repo");
  11:     std::fs::create_dir(&repo).expect("create repo subdir");
  12:     let src = Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))
  13:         .join("tests")
  14:         .join("fixtures")
  15:         .join("tiny_repo");
  16:     copy_dir_recursive(&src, &repo).expect("copy fixture");
  17:     (dir, repo)
  18: }
  19: 
  20: fn copy_dir_recursive(src: &Path, dst: &Path) -> std::io::Result<()> {
  21:     for entry in std::fs::read_dir(src)? {
  22:         let entry = entry?;
  23:         let dest = dst.join(entry.file_name());
  24:         if entry.file_type()?.is_dir() {
  25:             std::fs::create_dir_all(&dest)?;
  26:             copy_dir_recursive(&entry.path(), &dest)?;
  27:         } else {
  28:             std::fs::copy(entry.path(), &dest)?;
  29:         }
  30:     }
  31:     Ok(())
  32: }

===== /tests/helpers/git_helpers.rs =====

   1: //! Shared helpers for tests that need a real git repository in a tempdir.
   2: //!
   3: //! `build_repo` is the workhorse: given a slice of `(commit_msg, files)`,
   4: //! it initialises a repo, applies each commit (creating, modifying, or
   5: //! deleting files based on the file content — empty content means "delete"),
   6: //! and assigns each commit a strictly-increasing fake timestamp so revwalk
   7: //! ordering is deterministic across machines and seconds.
   8: 
   9: use git2::{IndexAddOption, Repository, Signature, Time};
  10: use std::fs;
  11: use std::path::Path;
  12: 
  13: /// Build a git repository in `dir`. Each entry is
  14: /// `(commit_msg, [(rel_path, content)])`. Empty `content` means
  15: /// "delete this file in this commit".
  16: pub fn build_repo(dir: &Path, commits: &[(&str, Vec<(&str, &str)>)]) {
  17:     let repo = Repository::init(dir).expect("git init");
  18:     let mut parent: Option<git2::Oid> = None;
  19:     // Start in 2024 so we never collide with the system clock.
  20:     let mut epoch: i64 = 1_700_000_000;
  21: 
  22:     for (msg, files) in commits {
  23:         for (rel, content) in files {
  24:             let full = dir.join(rel);
  25:             if let Some(p) = full.parent() {
  26:                 fs::create_dir_all(p).expect("mkdir parent");
  27:             }
  28:             if content.is_empty() {
  29:                 let _ = fs::remove_file(&full);
  30:             } else {
  31:                 fs::write(&full, content).expect("write file");
  32:             }
  33:         }
  34: 
  35:         let mut idx = repo.index().expect("index");
  36:         idx.add_all(["*"].iter(), IndexAddOption::DEFAULT, None).expect("add_all");
  37:         idx.update_all(["*"].iter(), None).expect("update_all");
  38:         idx.write().expect("write index");
  39:         let tree_oid = idx.write_tree().expect("write_tree");
  40:         let tree = repo.find_tree(tree_oid).expect("find_tree");
  41: 
  42:         let sig = Signature::new("Tester", "tester@example.com", &Time::new(epoch, 0))
  43:             .expect("sig");
  44:         epoch += 60;
  45: 
  46:         let parents: Vec<git2::Commit> = parent
  47:             .iter()
  48:             .filter_map(|oid| repo.find_commit(*oid).ok())
  49:             .collect();
  50:         let parents_ref: Vec<&git2::Commit> = parents.iter().collect();
  51: 
  52:         let oid = repo
  53:             .commit(Some("HEAD"), &sig, &sig, msg, &tree, &parents_ref)
  54:             .expect("commit");
  55:         parent = Some(oid);
  56:     }
  57: }

===== /tests/helpers/mock_api.rs =====

   1: use wiremock::matchers::{body_string_contains, method, path};
   2: use wiremock::{Mock, MockServer, ResponseTemplate};
   3: 
   4: /// A wiremock server pre-loaded with routes that mimic the Anthropic API.
   5: ///
   6: /// Routing uses unique keywords in each prompt template:
   7: ///  - `skeleton`     → stub response     `{"stub": "..."}`
   8: ///  - `intermediate` → versions response `{"versions": [...]}`
   9: ///  - (catch-all)    → describe response `{"description": "..."}`
  10: ///
  11: /// Mocks are registered lowest-priority first; the most-specific matchers
  12: /// (registered last) win when multiple mocks match the same request.
  13: pub struct MockApiServer {
  14:     inner: MockServer,
  15: }
  16: 
  17: impl MockApiServer {
  18:     pub async fn start() -> Self {
  19:         let inner = MockServer::start().await;
  20: 
  21:         // wiremock 0.6: FIRST registered mock wins when multiple matchers match.
  22:         // Register most-specific first, catch-all last.
  23:         //
  24:         // Discriminator: each prompt ends with its output-format specification.
  25:         // When the prompt is JSON-serialised into the request body the double-
  26:         // quotes are escaped, so `{"stub":` → `\"stub\":` in the raw body bytes.
  27:         // That escaped pattern is unique to each prompt template.
  28: 
  29:         // GenerateStub: output format ends with `{"stub": ...}` → body contains `\"stub\":`
  30:         Mock::given(method("POST"))
  31:             .and(path("/v1/messages"))
  32:             .and(body_string_contains(r#"\"stub\":"#))
  33:             .respond_with(
  34:                 ResponseTemplate::new(200).set_body_json(&serde_json::json!({
  35:                     "content": [{
  36:                         "type": "text",
  37:                         "text": r#"{"stub": "// stub placeholder\npub fn placeholder() { todo!() }"}"#
  38:                     }]
  39:                 })),
  40:             )
  41:             .mount(&inner)
  42:             .await;
  43: 
  44:         // GenerateIntermediates: output format ends with `{"versions": [...]}` → `\"versions\":`
  45:         Mock::given(method("POST"))
  46:             .and(path("/v1/messages"))
  47:             .and(body_string_contains(r#"\"versions\":"#))
  48:             .respond_with(
  49:                 ResponseTemplate::new(200).set_body_json(&serde_json::json!({
  50:                     "content": [{
  51:                         "type": "text",
  52:                         "text": r#"{"versions": ["// intermediate version 1"]}"#
  53:                     }]
  54:                 })),
  55:             )
  56:             .mount(&inner)
  57:             .await;
  58: 
  59:         // DescribeTransition: catch-all (registered last — only reached if neither above matched)
  60:         Mock::given(method("POST"))
  61:             .and(path("/v1/messages"))
  62:             .respond_with(
  63:                 ResponseTemplate::new(200).set_body_json(&serde_json::json!({
  64:                     "content": [{
  65:                         "type": "text",
  66:                         "text": r#"{"description": "Implementing core functionality."}"#
  67:                     }]
  68:                 })),
  69:             )
  70:             .mount(&inner)
  71:             .await;
  72: 
  73:         Self { inner }
  74:     }
  75: 
  76:     /// Base URL for the mock server, e.g. `http://127.0.0.1:12345`.
  77:     pub fn base_url(&self) -> String {
  78:         self.inner.uri()
  79:     }
  80: 
  81:     /// All requests the server has received since startup, in arrival order.
  82:     /// Returns `None` if request recording is disabled — wiremock keeps it on
  83:     /// by default. Used by tests that need to assert on request bodies (e.g.
  84:     /// to verify Phase 1 → Phase 2 data flow).
  85:     pub async fn received_requests(&self) -> Option<Vec<wiremock::Request>> {
  86:         self.inner.received_requests().await
  87:     }
  88: }

===== /tests/helpers/mod.rs =====

   1: pub mod fixture_repo;
   2: pub mod git_helpers;
   3: pub mod mock_api;

===== /tests/integration_test.rs =====

   1: //! Integration tests for the AI orchestration pipeline.
   2: //!
   3: //! These tests use a wiremock server in place of the real Anthropic API.
   4: //! Because they all write to the `GHOSTWRITER_LLM_BASE_URL` environment
   5: //! variable, they must run serially (`#[serial]`).
   6: //!
   7: //! Run with:
   8: //!   cargo test --test integration_test
   9: 
  10: mod helpers;
  11: 
  12: use helpers::{fixture_repo, mock_api};
  13: use ghostwriter::config::Config;
  14: use ghostwriter::orchestrator;
  15: use serial_test::serial;
  16: 
  17: // ─── Mock API round-trip ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  18: 
  19: /// Run orchestrate() against the tiny_repo fixture through the mock API and
  20: /// verify the resulting script has the expected shape.
  21: #[tokio::test]
  22: #[serial]
  23: async fn test_orchestrate_with_mock_api() {
  24:     let mock = mock_api::MockApiServer::start().await;
  25:     let (_dir, repo) = fixture_repo::copy_tiny_repo();
  26: 
  27:     std::env::set_var("GHOSTWRITER_LLM_BASE_URL", mock.base_url());
  28: 
  29:     let cfg = Config::default();
  30:     let script = orchestrator::orchestrate(&repo, None, &cfg, "test-key")
  31:         .await
  32:         .expect("orchestrate failed");
  33: 
  34:     assert!(!script.stages.is_empty(), "expected at least one stage");
  35:     // The project name is derived from the directory name.
  36:     assert_eq!(script.project_name, "tiny_repo");
  37:     // Every stage must have at least one action.
  38:     for stage in &script.stages {
  39:         assert!(!stage.actions.is_empty(), "stage '{}' has no actions", stage.name);
  40:     }
  41: }
  42: 
  43: // ─── Cache hit ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  44: 
  45: /// Second orchestrate() call with an existing cache file must return the same
  46: /// script without making any API calls.
  47: #[tokio::test]
  48: #[serial]
  49: async fn test_orchestrate_cache_hit() {
  50:     let mock = mock_api::MockApiServer::start().await;
  51:     let (_dir, repo) = fixture_repo::copy_tiny_repo();
  52:     let cache_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
  53:     let cache_path = cache_dir.path().join("script.json");
  54: 
  55:     std::env::set_var("GHOSTWRITER_LLM_BASE_URL", mock.base_url());
  56: 
  57:     let cfg = Config::default();
  58: 
  59:     // First call: generates and caches.
  60:     let script1 = orchestrator::orchestrate(&repo, Some(&cache_path), &cfg, "test-key")
  61:         .await
  62:         .expect("first orchestrate failed");
  63: 
  64:     assert!(cache_path.exists(), "cache file should have been written");
  65: 
  66:     // Second call with a bogus API key — must succeed from cache without hitting the mock.
  67:     let script2 = orchestrator::orchestrate(&repo, Some(&cache_path), &cfg, "bogus-key")
  68:         .await
  69:         .expect("second orchestrate (cache hit) failed");
  70: 
  71:     assert_eq!(
  72:         serde_json::to_string(&script1).unwrap(),
  73:         serde_json::to_string(&script2).unwrap(),
  74:         "cached script should be byte-identical to the original"
  75:     );
  76: }
  77: 
  78: // ─── LLM JSON retry ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  79: 
  80: /// llm::call() must retry once when the API returns non-JSON and succeed on
  81: /// the second attempt.  The retry prompt contains a unique phrase that the
  82: /// mock server uses to distinguish the two requests.
  83: #[tokio::test]
  84: #[serial]
  85: async fn test_llm_retries_on_non_json() {
  86:     use wiremock::matchers::{body_string_contains, method, path};
  87:     use wiremock::{Mock, MockServer, ResponseTemplate};
  88: 
  89:     let server = MockServer::start().await;
  90: 
  91:     // wiremock 0.6: FIRST registered wins. Specific matchers first, catch-all last.
  92: 
  93:     // Retry request (contains the error hint phrase) → valid JSON (registered first)
  94:     Mock::given(method("POST"))
  95:         .and(path("/v1/messages"))
  96:         .and(body_string_contains("previous response was not valid JSON"))
  97:         .respond_with(
  98:             ResponseTemplate::new(200).set_body_json(&serde_json::json!({
  99:                 "content": [{"type": "text", "text": r#"{"stub": "// retried successfully"}"#}]
 100:             })),
 101:         )
 102:         .mount(&server)
 103:         .await;
 104: 
 105:     // First request catch-all → non-JSON (registered last)
 106:     Mock::given(method("POST"))
 107:         .and(path("/v1/messages"))
 108:         .respond_with(
 109:             ResponseTemplate::new(200).set_body_json(&serde_json::json!({
 110:                 "content": [{"type": "text", "text": "This is not JSON at all."}]
 111:             })),
 112:         )
 113:         .mount(&server)
 114:         .await;
 115: 
 116:     std::env::set_var("GHOSTWRITER_LLM_BASE_URL", server.uri());
 117: 
 118:     let result = ghostwriter::orchestrator::llm::call("test prompt", "test-key").await;
 119:     assert!(result.is_ok(), "call should succeed after retry: {:?}", result);
 120: 
 121:     let json: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(&result.unwrap()).unwrap();
 122:     assert_eq!(json["stub"], "// retried successfully");
 123: }
 124: 
 125: // ─── Two-phase data-flow ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 126: 
 127: /// Phase 1 (`GenerateStub`) and `DescribeTransition` requests run first; the
 128: /// stubs they return must be threaded into Phase 2 (`GenerateIntermediates`)
 129: /// prompts. This test verifies that linkage by:
 130: ///
 131: ///   1. Running orchestrate against the mock server.
 132: ///   2. Pulling all recorded request bodies.
 133: ///   3. Asserting that at least one Phase 2 request body contains the literal
 134: ///      stub text the mock returned in Phase 1.
 135: ///
 136: /// If a future refactor accidentally feeds an empty / wrong stub into
 137: /// Phase 2, this test fails — that's the single bug class the existing
 138: /// `test_orchestrate_with_mock_api` cannot catch.
 139: #[tokio::test]
 140: #[serial]
 141: async fn test_phase1_stub_flows_into_phase2_prompt() {
 142:     // The literal stub string the mock returns for every GenerateStub request.
 143:     // Must match what's in tests/helpers/mock_api.rs.
 144:     const PHASE1_STUB: &str = "// stub placeholder\npub fn placeholder() { todo!() }";
 145: 
 146:     let mock = mock_api::MockApiServer::start().await;
 147:     let (_dir, repo) = fixture_repo::copy_tiny_repo();
 148: 
 149:     std::env::set_var("GHOSTWRITER_LLM_BASE_URL", mock.base_url());
 150: 
 151:     let cfg = Config::default();
 152:     let _ = orchestrator::orchestrate(&repo, None, &cfg, "test-key")
 153:         .await
 154:         .expect("orchestrate failed");
 155: 
 156:     let requests = mock
 157:         .received_requests()
 158:         .await
 159:         .expect("wiremock should have recorded requests");
 160:     assert!(!requests.is_empty(), "expected at least one request");
 161: 
 162:     // Partition requests by which prompt template they correspond to. The
 163:     // body discriminator matches what `MockApiServer` uses for routing —
 164:     // see tests/helpers/mock_api.rs for why these escaped-quote substrings
 165:     // are unique per template.
 166:     let mut phase1_count = 0;
 167:     let mut phase2_with_stub = 0;
 168:     let mut phase2_total = 0;
 169:     for r in &requests {
 170:         let body = String::from_utf8_lossy(&r.body);
 171:         let is_phase1 = body.contains(r#"\"stub\":"#);
 172:         let is_phase2 = body.contains(r#"\"versions\":"#);
 173:         if is_phase1 {
 174:             phase1_count += 1;
 175:         }
 176:         if is_phase2 {
 177:             phase2_total += 1;
 178:             // Phase 2 prompt for a given file must contain the stub returned
 179:             // by Phase 1 for that same file. The mock returns one canned
 180:             // PHASE1_STUB for every file, so all Phase 2 prompts must contain
 181:             // it. The stub text appears in the prompt as a JSON-escaped
 182:             // string — `\n` escapes and quotes don't interfere with our
 183:             // substring check on a single ASCII line of the placeholder.
 184:             if body.contains("placeholder()") {
 185:                 phase2_with_stub += 1;
 186:             }
 187:         }
 188:     }
 189: 
 190:     assert!(phase1_count > 0,
 191:             "expected at least one Phase 1 (stub) request, got: {phase1_count}");
 192:     assert!(phase2_total > 0,
 193:             "expected at least one Phase 2 (intermediates) request — \
 194:              tiny_repo's analyzer should produce non-trivial files");
 195:     assert_eq!(
 196:         phase2_with_stub, phase2_total,
 197:         "every Phase 2 prompt must include the Phase 1 stub text \
 198:          (saw {phase2_with_stub}/{phase2_total} containing the stub marker)"
 199:     );
 200:     let _ = PHASE1_STUB; // referenced in the comment above for traceability
 201: }
 202: 
 203: // ─── Cache miss → cache hit round-trip ───────────────────────────────────────
 204: 
 205: /// First call writes the cache; second call (with bogus key, irrelevant
 206: /// because the cache short-circuits) must produce byte-identical script.
 207: /// Catches regressions where cache write succeeds but writes a non-loadable
 208: /// file.
 209: #[tokio::test]
 210: #[serial]
 211: async fn test_cache_miss_writes_loadable_file() {
 212:     let mock = mock_api::MockApiServer::start().await;
 213:     let (_dir, repo) = fixture_repo::copy_tiny_repo();
 214:     let cache_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
 215:     let cache_path = cache_dir.path().join("script.json");
 216: 
 217:     std::env::set_var("GHOSTWRITER_LLM_BASE_URL", mock.base_url());
 218: 
 219:     let cfg = Config::default();
 220:     let first = orchestrator::orchestrate(&repo, Some(&cache_path), &cfg, "test-key")
 221:         .await
 222:         .expect("first orchestrate");
 223:     assert!(cache_path.exists(), "cache file must exist after orchestrate");
 224:     let cache_bytes = std::fs::read(&cache_path).unwrap();
 225:     assert!(!cache_bytes.is_empty(), "cache file must not be empty");
 226: 
 227:     // Second call: bogus key, should not call the API at all.
 228:     let second = orchestrator::orchestrate(&repo, Some(&cache_path), &cfg, "definitely-bogus")
 229:         .await
 230:         .expect("second orchestrate (cache hit)");
 231: 
 232:     assert_eq!(
 233:         serde_json::to_string(&first).unwrap(),
 234:         serde_json::to_string(&second).unwrap(),
 235:         "cache hit must reproduce the cached script byte-for-byte"
 236:     );
 237: }
 238: 
 239: // ─── Golden file ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 240: 
 241: /// Script generated from tiny_repo must match the stored golden JSON.
 242: ///
 243: /// To regenerate: `UPDATE_GOLDEN=1 cargo test --test integration_test test_golden_tiny_repo`
 244: #[tokio::test]
 245: #[serial]
 246: async fn test_golden_tiny_repo() {
 247:     let mock = mock_api::MockApiServer::start().await;
 248:     let (_dir, repo) = fixture_repo::copy_tiny_repo();
 249: 
 250:     std::env::set_var("GHOSTWRITER_LLM_BASE_URL", mock.base_url());
 251: 
 252:     let cfg = Config::default();
 253:     let script = orchestrator::orchestrate(&repo, None, &cfg, "test-key")
 254:         .await
 255:         .expect("orchestrate failed");
 256: 
 257:     let actual = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&script).expect("serialise script");
 258: 
 259:     let golden_path = std::path::Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))
 260:         .join("tests")
 261:         .join("golden")
 262:         .join("tiny_repo_script.json");
 263: 
 264:     if std::env::var("UPDATE_GOLDEN").is_ok() {
 265:         std::fs::create_dir_all(golden_path.parent().unwrap()).unwrap();
 266:         std::fs::write(&golden_path, &actual).expect("write golden file");
 267:         return; // nothing to assert — caller will commit the new file
 268:     }
 269: 
 270:     let expected = std::fs::read_to_string(&golden_path).unwrap_or_else(|_| {
 271:         panic!(
 272:             "golden file missing — run: UPDATE_GOLDEN=1 cargo test --test integration_test test_golden_tiny_repo"
 273:         )
 274:     });
 275: 
 276:     assert_eq!(actual, expected, "script does not match golden");
 277: }

===== /tests/repo_test.rs =====

   1: //! Integration tests for `repo::fetch` routing and URL normalisation.
   2: //!
   3: //! `repo::fetch` decides between three branches based on the input string:
   4: //!  - local path (starts with `/`, `.`, or `~`) → returns the path verbatim
   5: //!  - HTTPS / SSH git URL → clones into a tempdir
   6: //!  - anything else → treated as a remote (clone will fail with a network
   7: //!    error)
   8: //!
   9: //! Local-path routing is the most-exercised production case (and the only
  10: //! one runnable without network); the other two are gated behind
  11: //! `GHOSTWRITER_NETWORK_TESTS=1`.
  12: 
  13: use ghostwriter::config::Config;
  14: use ghostwriter::repo;
  15: 
  16: // ─── Local-path routing ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  17: 
  18: /// Absolute local path → fetch returns the same path, no clone, no network.
  19: #[tokio::test]
  20: async fn fetch_absolute_local_path_returns_input_unchanged() {
  21:     let tmp = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
  22:     let abs = tmp.path().to_path_buf();
  23:     let cfg = Config::default();
  24: 
  25:     let result = repo::fetch(abs.to_str().unwrap(), &cfg)
  26:         .await
  27:         .expect("fetch local path");
  28:     assert_eq!(result, abs);
  29: }
  30: 
  31: #[tokio::test]
  32: async fn fetch_relative_local_path_returns_input_unchanged() {
  33:     // Use `./.` so the path exists and is unambiguous.
  34:     let cfg = Config::default();
  35:     let result = repo::fetch("./.", &cfg).await.expect("fetch ./");
  36:     assert_eq!(result, std::path::PathBuf::from("./."));
  37: }
  38: 
  39: #[tokio::test]
  40: async fn fetch_home_path_returns_input_unchanged() {
  41:     // Don't try to read `~/...` — just verify routing returns the literal.
  42:     let cfg = Config::default();
  43:     let result = repo::fetch("~/nonexistent/should/not/clone", &cfg)
  44:         .await
  45:         .expect("fetch ~ path routing");
  46:     assert_eq!(
  47:         result,
  48:         std::path::PathBuf::from("~/nonexistent/should/not/clone")
  49:     );
  50: }
  51: 
  52: // (URL-normalisation unit tests live in src/repo/github.rs — the `github`
  53: //  module is private to the repo module and not reachable from integration
  54: //  tests.)
  55: 
  56: // ─── Network-gated clone (skipped by default) ───────────────────────────────
  57: 
  58: /// Real HTTPS clone of a tiny public repo. Gated by env var so CI doesn't
  59: /// have to opt in. The repo is one of GitHub's official "hello world"
  60: /// fixtures — small and stable.
  61: #[tokio::test]
  62: #[ignore = "network — run with GHOSTWRITER_NETWORK_TESTS=1 cargo test -- --include-ignored"]
  63: async fn fetch_https_clones_to_tempdir() {
  64:     if std::env::var("GHOSTWRITER_NETWORK_TESTS").is_err() {
  65:         return;
  66:     }
  67:     let cfg = Config::default();
  68:     let result = repo::fetch("https://github.com/octocat/Hello-World.git", &cfg)
  69:         .await
  70:         .expect("clone Hello-World");
  71:     assert!(result.exists(), "clone destination must exist");
  72:     assert!(result.join("README").exists() || result.join("README.md").exists(),
  73:             "clone should contain a README");
  74: }

===== /tests/script_roundtrip_test.rs =====

   1: //! `DevelopmentScript` JSON round-trip integration tests.
   2: //!
   3: //! Builds a script with one of every `Action` variant (`CreateFile`,
   4: //! `EditFile`, `DeleteFile`, `MoveCursor`, `RunCommand`, `ThinkPause`),
   5: //! serialises to JSON, deserialises, and verifies byte-identity.
   6: //!
   7: //! This is belt-and-braces against future serde rename / shape changes —
   8: //! a regression here breaks every cached `script.json` on disk and silently
   9: //! routes new runs through orchestrate again.
  10: 
  11: use ghostwriter::orchestrator::script::{Action, DevelopmentScript, Edit, Stage};
  12: use std::path::PathBuf;
  13: 
  14: fn one_of_each_action() -> DevelopmentScript {
  15:     DevelopmentScript {
  16:         project_name: "rt_test".to_string(),
  17:         repo_summary: "all six Action variants".to_string(),
  18:         stages: vec![Stage {
  19:             name: "all_actions".into(),
  20:             description: "every variant once".into(),
  21:             actions: vec![
  22:                 Action::CreateFile {
  23:                     path: PathBuf::from("src/main.rs"),
  24:                     stub: "fn main() { todo!() }".into(),
  25:                     intermediates: vec![
  26:                         "fn main() { let x = 1; todo!() }".into(),
  27:                         "fn main() { let x = 1; println!(\"{x}\"); todo!() }".into(),
  28:                     ],
  29:                     final_content: "fn main() { let x = 1; println!(\"{x}\"); }".into(),
  30:                     final_edits: vec![Edit {
  31:                         find: "todo!()".into(),
  32:                         replace: "".into(),
  33:                     }],
  34:                 },
  35:                 Action::EditFile {
  36:                     path: PathBuf::from("src/lib.rs"),
  37:                     edits: vec![
  38:                         Edit { find: "old".into(), replace: "new".into() },
  39:                         Edit { find: "alpha".into(), replace: "beta".into() },
  40:                     ],
  41:                 },
  42:                 Action::DeleteFile {
  43:                     path: PathBuf::from("Cargo.lock"),
  44:                 },
  45:                 Action::MoveCursor {
  46:                     path: PathBuf::from("src/main.rs"),
  47:                     line: 42,
  48:                 },
  49:                 Action::RunCommand {
  50:                     command: "cargo build".into(),
  51:                     cwd: Some(PathBuf::from("crates/foo")),
  52:                     expect_success: true,
  53:                 },
  54:                 Action::ThinkPause {
  55:                     min_ms: 800,
  56:                     max_ms: 2_000,
  57:                 },
  58:             ],
  59:         }],
  60:     }
  61: }
  62: 
  63: // ─── Round-trip ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  64: 
  65: #[test]
  66: fn json_serialise_then_deserialise_preserves_all_action_variants() {
  67:     let original = one_of_each_action();
  68:     let json = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&original).expect("serialise");
  69:     let restored: DevelopmentScript =
  70:         serde_json::from_str(&json).expect("deserialise");
  71: 
  72:     let original_json = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&original).unwrap();
  73:     let restored_json = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&restored).unwrap();
  74:     assert_eq!(original_json, restored_json,
  75:                "round-trip must produce byte-identical JSON");
  76: }
  77: 
  78: // ─── Tag/discriminator stability ────────────────────────────────────────────
  79: 
  80: /// `Action` uses `#[serde(tag = "type", rename_all = "snake_case")]`. If
  81: /// anyone changes the tag name or the variant naming convention, every
  82: /// cached script and integration test breaks. Pin the wire format here.
  83: #[test]
  84: fn action_tag_field_is_named_type() {
  85:     let val = serde_json::to_value(&Action::DeleteFile {
  86:         path: PathBuf::from("x"),
  87:     }).unwrap();
  88:     assert!(val.get("type").is_some(),
  89:             "Action tag must be the field 'type', got: {val}");
  90: }
  91: 
  92: #[test]
  93: fn action_variants_serialise_to_snake_case_tags() {
  94:     let cases = [
  95:         (Action::CreateFile {
  96:             path: PathBuf::from("x"),
  97:             stub: "".into(),
  98:             intermediates: vec![],
  99:             final_content: "".into(),
 100:             final_edits: vec![],
 101:         }, "create_file"),
 102:         (Action::EditFile {
 103:             path: PathBuf::from("x"),
 104:             edits: vec![],
 105:         }, "edit_file"),
 106:         (Action::DeleteFile { path: PathBuf::from("x") }, "delete_file"),
 107:         (Action::MoveCursor {
 108:             path: PathBuf::from("x"), line: 1,
 109:         }, "move_cursor"),
 110:         (Action::RunCommand {
 111:             command: "cmd".into(), cwd: None, expect_success: true,
 112:         }, "run_command"),
 113:         (Action::ThinkPause { min_ms: 0, max_ms: 1 }, "think_pause"),
 114:     ];
 115: 
 116:     for (action, expected_tag) in cases {
 117:         let val = serde_json::to_value(&action).unwrap();
 118:         let tag = val.get("type")
 119:             .and_then(|v| v.as_str())
 120:             .expect("missing or non-string `type`");
 121:         assert_eq!(tag, expected_tag,
 122:                    "wrong tag for variant {action:?}: got {tag}, want {expected_tag}");
 123:     }
 124: }
 125: 
 126: // ─── DevelopmentScript::save / load round-trip via disk ─────────────────────
 127: 
 128: #[test]
 129: fn script_save_and_load_round_trip_via_disk() {
 130:     let original = one_of_each_action();
 131:     let tmp = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
 132:     let path = tmp.path().join("script.json");
 133: 
 134:     original.save(&path).expect("save");
 135:     assert!(path.exists());
 136: 
 137:     let loaded = DevelopmentScript::load(&path).expect("load");
 138:     assert_eq!(
 139:         serde_json::to_string(&original).unwrap(),
 140:         serde_json::to_string(&loaded).unwrap(),
 141:         "disk round-trip must produce byte-identical script"
 142:     );
 143: }
 144: 
 145: #[test]
 146: fn empty_script_round_trips() {
 147:     let s = DevelopmentScript {
 148:         project_name: "empty".into(),
 149:         repo_summary: "".into(),
 150:         stages: vec![],
 151:     };
 152:     let json = serde_json::to_string(&s).unwrap();
 153:     let back: DevelopmentScript = serde_json::from_str(&json).unwrap();
 154:     assert!(back.stages.is_empty());
 155: }

===== /tests/session_test.rs =====

   1: //! End-to-end session lifecycle tests.
   2: //!
   3: //! Covers the resume path that was previously uncovered: write a session
   4: //! to disk in various lifecycle states (Started / InProgress / Completed /
   5: //! Interrupted / Failed), reload, verify state transitions and resume
   6: //! decision logic match expectations. Also smoke-tests `ghostwriter resume`
   7: //! via the CLI for the "no session" and "already complete" error paths.
   8: 
   9: use ghostwriter::session::{SessionState, SessionStatus};
  10: use std::path::PathBuf;
  11: use std::process::Command;
  12: use tempfile::TempDir;
  13: 
  14: const BIN: &str = env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_ghostwriter");
  15: 
  16: fn fake_stages() -> Vec<String> {
  17:     vec![
  18:         "skeleton".into(),
  19:         "types".into(),
  20:         "impl_0".into(),
  21:         "tests".into(),
  22:     ]
  23: }
  24: 
  25: fn fresh_session(dest: &std::path::Path) -> SessionState {
  26:     SessionState::new(
  27:         "https://github.com/test/repo",
  28:         dest.to_path_buf(),
  29:         dest.join("source"),
  30:         dest.join(".ghostwriter/script.json"),
  31:         Some(42),
  32:         75,
  33:         false,
  34:         &fake_stages(),
  35:     )
  36: }
  37: 
  38: // ── Lifecycle: fresh → in-progress → completed via complete_stage ───────────
  39: 
  40: #[test]
  41: fn lifecycle_progresses_through_complete_stage() {
  42:     let tmp = TempDir::new().unwrap();
  43:     let mut s = fresh_session(tmp.path());
  44:     s.save().unwrap();
  45: 
  46:     // After save: Started, no completed stages, next_stage = first.
  47:     let loaded = SessionState::load(tmp.path()).unwrap();
  48:     assert_eq!(loaded.checkpoint.status, SessionStatus::Started);
  49:     assert!(loaded.checkpoint.completed_stages.is_empty());
  50:     assert_eq!(loaded.checkpoint.next_stage.as_deref(), Some("skeleton"));
  51: 
  52:     // Complete the first stage.
  53:     s.complete_stage("skeleton", &fake_stages()).unwrap();
  54:     let loaded = SessionState::load(tmp.path()).unwrap();
  55:     assert_eq!(loaded.checkpoint.status, SessionStatus::InProgress);
  56:     assert_eq!(loaded.checkpoint.completed_stages, vec!["skeleton"]);
  57:     assert_eq!(loaded.checkpoint.next_stage.as_deref(), Some("types"));
  58: 
  59:     // Complete remaining stages.
  60:     s.complete_stage("types",  &fake_stages()).unwrap();
  61:     s.complete_stage("impl_0", &fake_stages()).unwrap();
  62:     s.complete_stage("tests",  &fake_stages()).unwrap();
  63: 
  64:     let loaded = SessionState::load(tmp.path()).unwrap();
  65:     assert_eq!(loaded.checkpoint.status, SessionStatus::Completed,
  66:                "all stages done must transition to Completed");
  67:     assert!(loaded.checkpoint.next_stage.is_none(),
  68:             "no pending next_stage when all complete");
  69: }
  70: 
  71: // ── resume_from_stage: override beats checkpoint, otherwise uses next_stage ──
  72: 
  73: #[test]
  74: fn resume_from_stage_with_override_beats_next_stage() {
  75:     let tmp = TempDir::new().unwrap();
  76:     let mut s = fresh_session(tmp.path());
  77:     s.complete_stage("skeleton", &fake_stages()).unwrap(); // next_stage = "types"
  78: 
  79:     // No override → use checkpoint's next_stage.
  80:     let target = s.resume_from_stage(None);
  81:     assert_eq!(target, Some("types"));
  82: 
  83:     // Override → wins regardless of checkpoint state.
  84:     let target = s.resume_from_stage(Some("impl_0"));
  85:     assert_eq!(target, Some("impl_0"));
  86: }
  87: 
  88: #[test]
  89: fn resume_from_stage_returns_none_when_complete() {
  90:     let tmp = TempDir::new().unwrap();
  91:     let mut s = fresh_session(tmp.path());
  92:     for st in fake_stages() {
  93:         s.complete_stage(&st, &fake_stages()).unwrap();
  94:     }
  95:     assert_eq!(s.resume_from_stage(None), None,
  96:                "complete session yields None for default resume target");
  97: }
  98: 
  99: // ── is_stage_done reflects completion list ──────────────────────────────────
 100: 
 101: #[test]
 102: fn is_stage_done_tracks_completed_stages() {
 103:     let tmp = TempDir::new().unwrap();
 104:     let mut s = fresh_session(tmp.path());
 105:     assert!(!s.is_stage_done("skeleton"));
 106:     s.complete_stage("skeleton", &fake_stages()).unwrap();
 107:     assert!(s.is_stage_done("skeleton"));
 108:     assert!(!s.is_stage_done("types"));
 109: }
 110: 
 111: // ── mark_interrupted / mark_failed transitions ──────────────────────────────
 112: 
 113: #[test]
 114: fn mark_interrupted_persists_status() {
 115:     let tmp = TempDir::new().unwrap();
 116:     let mut s = fresh_session(tmp.path());
 117:     s.save().unwrap();
 118:     s.mark_interrupted().unwrap();
 119: 
 120:     let loaded = SessionState::load(tmp.path()).unwrap();
 121:     assert_eq!(loaded.checkpoint.status, SessionStatus::Interrupted);
 122: }
 123: 
 124: #[test]
 125: fn mark_failed_persists_status() {
 126:     let tmp = TempDir::new().unwrap();
 127:     let mut s = fresh_session(tmp.path());
 128:     s.save().unwrap();
 129:     s.mark_failed().unwrap();
 130: 
 131:     let loaded = SessionState::load(tmp.path()).unwrap();
 132:     assert_eq!(loaded.checkpoint.status, SessionStatus::Failed);
 133: }
 134: 
 135: // ── exists() returns false for missing dirs ─────────────────────────────────
 136: 
 137: #[test]
 138: fn exists_returns_false_when_no_session_file_present() {
 139:     let tmp = TempDir::new().unwrap();
 140:     assert!(!SessionState::exists(tmp.path()));
 141: }
 142: 
 143: // ── partial-completion resume: save mid-flight, reload, continue ────────────
 144: 
 145: #[test]
 146: fn save_load_round_trip_preserves_completed_stages_in_order() {
 147:     let tmp = TempDir::new().unwrap();
 148:     let mut s = fresh_session(tmp.path());
 149:     s.complete_stage("skeleton", &fake_stages()).unwrap();
 150:     s.complete_stage("types",    &fake_stages()).unwrap();
 151: 
 152:     let loaded = SessionState::load(tmp.path()).unwrap();
 153:     assert_eq!(loaded.checkpoint.completed_stages, vec!["skeleton", "types"]);
 154:     assert_eq!(loaded.checkpoint.next_stage.as_deref(), Some("impl_0"));
 155:     assert_eq!(loaded.checkpoint.status, SessionStatus::InProgress);
 156:     // Round-trip preserved everything else too.
 157:     assert_eq!(loaded.session.repo, "https://github.com/test/repo");
 158:     assert_eq!(loaded.run.seed, Some(42));
 159:     assert_eq!(loaded.run.wpm_effective, 75);
 160: }
 161: 
 162: // ── CLI: ghostwriter resume on a missing session bails with a clear msg ─────
 163: 
 164: #[test]
 165: fn cli_resume_no_session_file_exits_with_error() {
 166:     let dest = TempDir::new().unwrap();
 167:     let output = Command::new(BIN)
 168:         .env("GHOSTWRITER_LOG", "ghostwriter=warn")
 169:         .args(["resume", "--dest", dest.path().to_str().unwrap()])
 170:         .output()
 171:         .expect("spawn ghostwriter resume");
 172: 
 173:     assert!(!output.status.success(), "expected non-zero exit");
 174:     let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr);
 175:     assert!(
 176:         stderr.contains("no session file") || stderr.contains("session.toml"),
 177:         "expected 'no session file' message, got stderr:\n{stderr}"
 178:     );
 179: }
 180: 
 181: // ── CLI: ghostwriter resume on a Completed session bails ────────────────────
 182: 
 183: #[test]
 184: fn cli_resume_completed_session_exits_with_error() {
 185:     let dest = TempDir::new().unwrap();
 186:     let mut s = fresh_session(dest.path());
 187:     for st in fake_stages() {
 188:         s.complete_stage(&st, &fake_stages()).unwrap();
 189:     }
 190: 
 191:     // Sanity: the file is on disk and status is Completed.
 192:     let loaded = SessionState::load(dest.path()).unwrap();
 193:     assert_eq!(loaded.checkpoint.status, SessionStatus::Completed);
 194:     let _ = PathBuf::from("");
 195: 
 196:     let output = Command::new(BIN)
 197:         .env("GHOSTWRITER_LOG", "ghostwriter=warn")
 198:         .args(["resume", "--dest", dest.path().to_str().unwrap()])
 199:         .output()
 200:         .expect("spawn ghostwriter resume");
 201: 
 202:     assert!(!output.status.success(), "completed session must not resume");
 203:     let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr);
 204:     assert!(
 205:         stderr.contains("complete") || stderr.contains("finished"),
 206:         "expected 'already complete' message, got stderr:\n{stderr}"
 207:     );
 208: }
